# @graphty/webgpu-graph-algorithms P0 -- reset and package skeleton Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Replace the 2025 scaffold with the `packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/` package skeleton (manifest, configs, scripts, CI lanes, the error class and constants, the test setup with the GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE policy, one device test per vitest project) so that both CI lanes run green and the owner can sign off gate G0.

**Architecture:** The package mirrors `packages/graph-io` file for file (spec 3.1): one public barrel, `./browser` and `./node` subpath entries, a three-project vitest config (node / node-limits / browser), plain-JS scripts, and a package-local ESLint config that enforces the layer rule with core `no-restricted-imports` zones plus a cycle test. Nothing above the device layer's constants exists yet; the walking skeleton is P1.

**Tech Stack:** TypeScript 5.9 (strict), WGSL, WebGPU via `webgpu@0.4.0` (Dawn) in Node 22 and Chromium 139 (Playwright) in the browser, `@graphty/graph-format` snapshots, vitest 3.2 (node / node-limits / browser projects, v8 coverage), fast-check, pnpm 10 workspace, vite 7 library bundle, ESLint 9 flat config, knip, GitHub Actions (ubuntu-latest lavapipe + SwiftShader lane; `gpu-linux-t4` lane).

**Spec:** `design/webgpu-acceleration-plan.md` (approved 2026-09-14; the last Review-log entry lists four contract corrections pending the owner's G0 confirmation) and the normative interface contract `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-14-webgpu-p0-p3-interfaces.md` (read its section 7 for this phase's task map, then the sections it cites for each file).

**Gate:** G0 (spec 13 row P0; contract 7.1).

**Tasks in this document:** P0-T1, P0-T2, P0-T3, P0-T4. The contract's section 7 table gives the dependency order and which tasks run in parallel (in one working tree; every file has exactly one owning task per phase).

## Global Constraints

Copied from the spec and the owner's rules; every task's requirements implicitly include this section.

- Spec rules for every phase (spec 13): (a) the gate is a list of tests and recorded MEASURED numbers green on the default lane AND the GPU lane before the next phase starts; (b) a phase adds only the primitives its slice needs; (c) every [X] number the phase touches is replaced by a measured one in `benchmarks/results/`; (d) nothing lands with `eslint-disable`, `@ts-expect-error` (outside negative type tests), non-ASCII, or a CPU fallback; (f) every phase that adds a kernel adds its sabotage mutations, its `inspect()` stage comparisons and its noise-floor row (spec 11.9).
- Project rule (`CLAUDE.md`): never create fallbacks if WebGPU is not supported -- `src/` contains no CPU path, no WebGL path, no software-adapter acceptance; the package throws.
- Owner rules: never run `git add`, `git commit` or `git push` (the owner commits from `tmp/commit-p<N>.sh`; a task ends with a Checkpoint, not a commit); never `sudo`; plain ASCII in every file, commit message and output (`--` for dashes, straight quotes); no `eslint-disable` / `@ts-expect-error`; temporary files under `./tmp/`; servers only on ports 9000-9099 (coverage preview 9058); write scripts to `./tmp/` instead of repeating inline one-liners; never add a `Co-Authored-By` or `Claude-Session` trailer anywhere.
- House style (spec 3.6; note 07 section 7): JSDoc on every export with `@param` / `@returns`, explicit return types, `.js` suffixes on relative imports, inline `import { type X, y }` qualifiers, no default exports, no `console.log` in `src/` (`console.warn` only for the residency warning), options `?: T | undefined`, absent output is `null`, results are `Uint32Array<ArrayBuffer>` / `Float32Array<ArrayBuffer>`, `INVALID_INDEX` is the only sentinel, never a bitwise operator on an arc index or byte offset, never a write into a view, never a zero-length binding, prettier 4 / 120 / all, knip clean.
- Layer rule (spec 3.2; contract lead a): device < context < memory < kernel < primitives < algorithms / layouts < accelerator; a lower layer never imports a higher one; `src/wgsl/**` is imported only by `kernels.ts`; enforced by the package `eslint.config.js` zones and `test/layers.test.ts`.
- Versions (spec 2.5): `webgpu@0.4.0` pinned until P-ENV; `@webgpu/types ^0.1.72`; `vitest` / `@vitest/browser ^3.2.4` (3.2.7 installed); `playwright ^1.54.1` (Chromium 139); `vite ^7`; `typescript 5.9.3`; `@graphty/graph-format` `workspace:^` in dependencies and `^0.1.0` in peerDependencies.
- Constants (spec 3.1, 5.2, 7.8): `WORKGROUP_SIZE = 256`, `MAX_WORKGROUPS_PER_DIM = 65535`, the 1D boundary is 16,776,960 items (NOT 2^24), `ARC_WINDOW_ALIGN = 64`, `STORAGE_ALIGN = 256`, `EXACT_MAX_NODES = 16384` until G3 re-fixes it; interpolated into the WGSL prelude, never retyped as literals.
- Test policy (spec 11.2, D19): the ONE variable `GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE` (unset = skip with a printed `E_NO_ADAPTER` reason; `any` / `hardware` / `<vendor>` = hard failure); a wrong result is never a skip; an uncaptured error fails the current test; a fresh adapter per device; `gpuScale()` = 1 on hardware, 1/50 on software adapters.
- Environment (contract section 8): commands run from `packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms` (workspace root `packages/`); default-lane env `GRAPHTY_GPU_ADAPTER=llvmpipe GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=any VK_DRIVER_FILES=/usr/share/vulkan/icd.d/lvp_icd.x86_64.json XDG_RUNTIME_DIR=/tmp`; local NVIDIA runs use `GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=hardware`; the browser project on NVIDIA needs `GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU=nvidia` and `LD_LIBRARY_PATH=/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/egl/root/usr/lib/x86_64-linux-gnu`; on SwiftShader `GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU=swiftshader`.
- Green check at the end of every task: `pnpm run build:all && pnpm run lint && (cd .. && pnpm exec knip) && pnpm exec vitest run --project=node` on NVIDIA and with the default-lane env where the task touched GPU code, plus `node scripts/run-browser-project.js` on both browser adapters where the task touched browser code.
- The interface contract is normative: a signature not declared in it does not exist; disagreements are resolved by changing the contract (and the spec's Review log) first.

Four PLAN DECISIONS govern this task (each restated where it applies):

- PLAN DECISION (Steps 4-5): plain `rm` / `mv`, not `git rm` / `git mv`, because the owner forbids any staging
  by an agent session; the deletions and the rename are staged by the owner's `git add -A` in
  `tmp/commit-p0.sh`, and rename detection on the unchanged blob gives the same history as `git mv`.
- PLAN DECISION (Step 7): the root `.gitignore` writes `**/benchmarks/out/` where contract 1.1 says
  `benchmarks/out/`, because the contract's spelling would ignore only `<root>/benchmarks/out/` and not the
  package's `benchmarks/out/` it exists for (verified with `git check-ignore -v` in a scratch repository).
- PLAN DECISION (Step 7): the root `.gitignore` adds `!packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/docs/research/review/probes/*.log`
  after `*.log`, because the contract's `*.log` line would otherwise ignore the four probe logs contract 1.1
  copies into the repository. Both `.gitignore` decisions are contract amendments: the P0 plan's assembler
  records them in contract 1.1 and the spec's Review log (the contract's "How to use" rule).
- PLAN DECISION (Step 6): no `docs/research/README.md` index is written (the contract declares no such file;
  the root `README.md` of Step 8 and P0-T3's package `CLAUDE.md` carry the pointers), the copies are
  byte-identical (never Prettier-formatted), and stale path citations of the old root location in
  `packages/MIGRATION_PROMPT.md` line 63, the spec (line 4380) and
  `packages/graph-format/test/audit/gpu-upload.test.ts` line 13 stay as they are (contract 1.1 lists those
  files as unchanged; the spec is edited only by the owner's Review-log appendices).

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the verification script (the failing test) (5 min)**

Write `/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/p0-t1-check.sh` with exactly this content (it lives
under the gitignored `tmp/` and is never committed; `R` may be overridden to check a copy of the tree):

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# P0-T1 verification: the repository root after the scaffold triage (contract 1.1, spec 13 row P0).
# Run: bash /home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/p0-t1-check.sh
# One PASS / FAIL line per assertion; exit 1 when any assertion failed. R may be overridden to check a copy.
set -u
R=${R:-/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms}
P=$R/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms
SRC=$R/tmp/webgpu-plan
fail=0
pass() { printf 'PASS %s\n' "$1"; }
failf() { printf 'FAIL %s\n' "$1"; fail=$((fail + 1)); }
# check DESCRIPTION COMMAND...: PASS when the command exits 0
check() {
    local desc=$1
    shift
    if "$@" >/dev/null 2>&1; then pass "$desc"; else failf "$desc"; fi
}
absent() { [ ! -e "$1" ] && [ ! -L "$1" ]; }
count_files() { find "$1" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type f 2>/dev/null | wc -l; }

# 1. The deleted scaffold (contract 1.1) and the moved report's old path.
for p in .env .env.example .husky .vscode dist examples eslint.config.js knip.json node_modules package.json \
    package-lock.json src test tsconfig.json vite.config.ts vitest.config.ts STRATEGY.md IMPLEMENTATION_CHECKLIST.md \
    .github/workflows/test.yml HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md; do
    check "root: $p absent" absent "$R/$p"
done
check "root: .github/ kept (P0-T2 writes ci.yml and gpu.yml there)" test -d "$R/.github"

# 2. Kept as is.
check "root: CLAUDE.md unchanged" git -C "$R" diff --quiet HEAD -- CLAUDE.md
check "root: design/webgpu-acceleration-plan.md present" test -f "$R/design/webgpu-acceleration-plan.md"
check "root: the contract present" test -f "$R/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-14-webgpu-p0-p3-interfaces.md"
check "packages/graph-format untouched" test -z "$(git -C "$R" status --short -- packages/graph-format)"
check "packages/graph-io untouched" test -z "$(git -C "$R" status --short -- packages/graph-io)"

# 3. The move: byte-identical to the committed report.
check "docs/HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md equals HEAD:HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md" \
    bash -c "git -C '$R' show HEAD:HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md | cmp -s - '$P/docs/HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md'"

# 4. The copies: every listed source byte-identical at its destination, nothing else copied.
for f in "$SRC"/0[1-7]-*.md "$SRC"/draft-A.md "$SRC"/draft-B.md "$SRC"/draft-C.md; do
    check "research: $(basename "$f") identical" cmp -s "$f" "$P/docs/research/$(basename "$f")"
done
for f in "$SRC"/review/*.md; do
    check "review: $(basename "$f") identical" cmp -s "$f" "$P/docs/research/review/$(basename "$f")"
done
for f in "$SRC"/review/probes/*.mjs "$SRC"/review/probes/*.log; do
    check "probes: $(basename "$f") identical" cmp -s "$f" "$P/docs/research/review/probes/$(basename "$f")"
done
check "research: exactly 10 files in docs/research/" test "$(count_files "$P/docs/research")" = 10
check "review: exactly 15 files in docs/research/review/" test "$(count_files "$P/docs/research/review")" = 15
check "probes: exactly 23 files in docs/research/review/probes/" test "$(count_files "$P/docs/research/review/probes")" = 23
check "probes: no subdirectory copied (nodom, nodom-verify)" \
    test -z "$(find "$P/docs/research/review/probes" -mindepth 1 -type d 2>/dev/null)"
check "research: no *.html copied (the spec dumps stay in tmp/)" test -z "$(find "$P/docs" -name '*.html' 2>/dev/null)"
check "docs: 49 files in total (the report + 48 copies; docs/decisions/ is P0-T4's)" \
    test "$(find "$P/docs" -path "$P/docs/decisions" -prune -o -type f -print 2>/dev/null | wc -l)" = 49

# 5. The root .gitignore: every rule of contract 1.1 effective, the committed probe logs not ignored.
for ig in tmp/x .tmp/x node_modules/x dist/x coverage/x a.tsbuildinfo benchmarks/out/x \
    packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/benchmarks/out/x packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/browser-results.json \
    packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/gpu-report.json x.log packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/x.log .DS_Store; do
    check "gitignore: $ig ignored" git -C "$R" check-ignore -q "$ig"
done
check "gitignore: the copied probe logs are NOT ignored" \
    bash -c "! git -C '$R' check-ignore -q packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/docs/research/review/probes/design-probe-nvidia.log"
check "gitignore: docs/research notes are NOT ignored" \
    bash -c "! git -C '$R' check-ignore -q packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/docs/research/01-layout-needs.md"
check "gitignore: 12 rule lines" test "$(grep -cv '^#\|^$' "$R/.gitignore")" = 12
for line in node_modules dist coverage tmp/ .tmp/ '*.tsbuildinfo' '**/benchmarks/out/' browser-results.json \
    gpu-report.json '*.log' '!packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/docs/research/review/probes/*.log' .DS_Store; do
    check "gitignore: line '$line' present" grep -Fxq -- "$line" "$R/.gitignore"
done

# 6. The root README: one screen, the five pointers, no scaffold leftovers.
check "README: at most 60 lines" test "$(wc -l < "$R/README.md")" -le 60
check "README: no line over 120 columns" test -z "$(awk 'length > 120' "$R/README.md")"
for ptr in packages/README.md design/webgpu-acceleration-plan.md docs/superpowers/plans/ \
    packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/docs/HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/docs/research/; do
    check "README: points at $ptr" grep -Fq -- "$ptr" "$R/README.md"
done
for gone in 'npm run dev' '.env.example' 'npm run test:webgpu' 'Node.js 18 or higher'; do
    check "README: scaffold text '$gone' gone" bash -c "! grep -Fq -- '$gone' '$R/README.md'"
done

# 7. Plain ASCII in the two rewritten files and the moved / copied documents.
check "ascii: README.md and .gitignore" bash -c "! grep -qP '[^\\x00-\\x7F]' '$R/README.md' '$R/.gitignore'"
check "ascii: docs/** (report, notes, drafts, review, probes)" \
    bash -c "! grep -rqP '[^\\x00-\\x7F]' '$P/docs'"

# 8. What the owner's commit script will stage: 26 tracked deletions, 4 tracked root files kept, 49 new files.
check "git: 26 tracked files deleted (25 scaffold files + the report's old path)" \
    test "$(git -C "$R" ls-files --deleted | wc -l)" = 26
check "git: the 4 kept tracked files outside packages/ are .gitignore CLAUDE.md README.md design/webgpu-acceleration-plan.md" \
    test "$(LC_ALL=C comm -23 <(git -C "$R" ls-files | grep -v '^packages/' | LC_ALL=C sort) <(git -C "$R" ls-files --deleted | LC_ALL=C sort) | tr '\n' ' ')" = \
    ".gitignore CLAUDE.md README.md design/webgpu-acceleration-plan.md "
check "git: 49 untracked files under packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/docs/ outside docs/decisions/ (P0-T4's)" \
    test "$(git -C "$R" status --short --untracked-files=all -- packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/docs \
        ':!packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/docs/decisions' | grep -c '^?? ')" = 49
check "git: README.md and .gitignore modified" \
    test "$(git -C "$R" status --short -- README.md .gitignore | grep -c '^ M ')" = 2

if [ "$fail" -ne 0 ]; then
    printf '%d assertion(s) FAILED\n' "$fail"
    exit 1
fi
printf 'all assertions passed\n'
```

The script makes 126 assertions. Hand-derived expectations it encodes: 25 tracked scaffold files + the report's

+ 10 + 15 + 23 copies = 49 files under `docs/` outside `docs/decisions/` (both 49-counts prune that directory:
P0-T4 writes `docs/decisions/G0.md` there before the P0 commit, and the script must stay green on the committed
tree); 12 non-comment lines in the new `.gitignore`.

- [ ] **Step 8: Rewrite the root `README.md` (5 min)**

Write `/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/README.md` with exactly this content (46 lines, no line
over 120 columns, plain ASCII; contract 1.1: "one screen -- what the repository stages, the package list,
pointers to packages/README.md, design/ and docs/superpowers/plans/"):

```markdown
# webgpu-graph-algorithms (graphty-org staging)

The public staging repository of graphty-org for packages that move into the graphty monorepo
(`graphty-org/graphty-monorepo`) once they are done. The repository root is not a package: the pnpm
workspace root is `packages/`, laid out like the monorepo root so that each package directory moves
with a plain `mv`.

## Packages

- `packages/graph-format/` -- `@graphty/graph-format`, the CSR graph snapshot (arena, views, columns).
  Done and audited; waiting for the move.
- `packages/graph-io/` -- `@graphty/graph-io`, importers and exporters for the snapshot. Done and
  audited; waiting for the move.
- `packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/` -- `@graphty/webgpu-graph-algorithms`, WebGPU graph algorithms
  and force-directed layouts over the snapshot, for Node (Dawn) and browsers. In progress: phases
  P0-P3 of the plan below.

## Where to look

- `packages/README.md` -- working in the workspace (`cd packages && pnpm install && pnpm -r run build:all`),
  the tool versions, the rehearsed move checklist.
- `design/webgpu-acceleration-plan.md` -- the WebGPU acceleration plan (the spec: runtime model, memory,
  kernels, layouts, integration, testing, CI, phases).
- `docs/superpowers/plans/` -- the normative P0-P3 interface contract
  (`2026-09-14-webgpu-p0-p3-interfaces.md`) and the per-phase implementation plans.
- `packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/docs/HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md` -- how headless Chromium reaches the
  NVIDIA GPU on the dev box (the `libEGL.so.1` root cause, the four flags, appendix D).
- `packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/docs/research/` -- the seven research notes, the three drafts and
  the review record (`review/`, with the probe scripts and their logs) that the plan cites as
  "note NN", "draft A/B/C" and "[M]".
- `packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/docs/decisions/` -- the phase-gate records (G0, G1, ...): measured
  numbers and sign-offs.
- `.github/workflows/` -- `ci.yml` (the default lane: Dawn on Mesa lavapipe plus Chromium on SwiftShader,
  GitHub-hosted runners) and `gpu.yml` (the NVIDIA T4 lane, gated by the `gpu` label).
- `tmp/` -- gitignored scratch: cloned repositories, papers, probes, the NetworkX venv, the owner's
  commit scripts.

## Rules that apply everywhere in this repository

- WebGPU is mandatory: no CPU, WebGL or software-adapter fallback ever lands in `src/` (`CLAUDE.md`).
  The test layer may skip with a printed reason only while `GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE` is unset.
- Plain ASCII in every file. Formatting is Prettier's (`packages/.prettierrc`); the lint rules are the
  monorepo's (`packages/eslint.config.js`).
- Agent sessions never `git add`, `git commit` or `git push`; the owner commits from `tmp/commit-p<N>.sh`.

License: MIT (`packages/*/LICENSE`).
```

  - Facts measured on the dev box while drafting this task (Dawn `webgpu@0.4.0`, RTX 4070 SUPER, driver 580.173.02), for P0-T3's tests and CLAUDE.md and P1-T7's file names: `adapter.info` on NVIDIA is `vendor: "nvidia", architecture: "lovelace", device: "nvidia-geforce-rtx-4070-super", description: "NVIDIA: 580.173.02 580.173.2.0", isFallbackAdapter: false, subgroupMinSize: 32, subgroupMaxSize: 32`, so `runnerClass(adapter.info)` is `nvidia-lovelace-driver580` (NOT `nvidia-ada-lovelace-...`: Dawn's architecture string has no `ada-`), and the dev-box baseline file of contract 6.4 is `benchmarks/results/nvidia-lovelace-driver580.json`; on llvmpipe it is `vendor: "mesa", architecture: "software", device: "llvmpipe-llvm-15-0-7-256-bits-", description: "llvmpipe: Mesa 23.2.1-1ubuntu3.1~22.04.3 (LLVM 15.0.7) 0.0.1", isFallbackAdapter: true, subgroupMinSize: 8, subgroupMaxSize: 8`, so the class is `mesa-software-driver23`; Dawn in Node reaches the NVIDIA ICD ONLY with `LD_LIBRARY_PATH=/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/egl/root/usr/lib/x86_64-linux-gnu` (without it Dawn silently picks llvmpipe -- the exact failure `GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=hardware` exists to catch); adapter limits NVIDIA `maxBufferSize 1099511627776, maxStorageBufferBindingSize 2147483644, maxStorageBuffersPerShaderStage 16`, llvmpipe `4294967295 / 134217728 / 16`, both `maxComputeWorkgroupsPerDimension 65535`; a default device on either has `maxBufferSize 268435456, maxStorageBufferBindingSize 134217728`. The declared runner-class rule takes the FIRST run of digits of `description`, so P0-T3's 4-row table must use Dawn's description format (`"NVIDIA: 580.173.02 580.173.2.0"` -> `driver580`); a description whose first digits are the model number (`"NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4070 SUPER ..."`) yields `driver4070` by the rule as declared.

P0-T3 depends on this task (it imports `scripts/gpu-policy.js`, runs under `vitest.config.ts` and lints under `eslint.config.js`); P0-T4 records the first `gpu.yml` dispatch.

PLAN DECISIONS made by this part (each keeps the contract's declared behaviour; the contract text itself is amended by the lead, not here):
1. `eslint.config.js`: the contract's two `no-restricted-globals` blocks overlap on every core file (both match `src/memory/x.ts`), and flat config REPLACES rule options, so the later block (`process` only) silently dropped `navigator` / `window` / `document` for the core -- verified with `ESLint.calculateConfigForFile`. The rule is written as three DISJOINT blocks (core minus the two entries: all four globals; `src/browser/**`: `process`; `src/node/**`: the DOM three). Same intent, now enforced; contract 2.4 needs the same three-block text (lead).
2. `gpu.yml`: the contract's `concurrency: { ..., cancel-in-progress: ${{ ... }} }` and `outputs: { run: ${{ ... }} }` are flow mappings containing `{` / `}` inside a plain scalar, which YAML forbids (PyYAML: "expected ',' or '}', but got '{'"; GitHub would reject the file). Both are written as block mappings with the same keys and values. `ci.yml` parses as given.
3. `scripts/run-browser-project.js`: GNU coreutils `timeout` exits 137 (128 + 9), not 124, when the `-k 10` KILL was needed after the TERM (timeout.c preserves the KILL status); both codes are "the limit hit" and get the JSON verdict of spec 11.6. Extra command-line arguments are forwarded to vitest (so `--passWithNoTests` gives a config smoke before any browser test exists); a stale `browser-results.json` is deleted before the run so an old file can never turn a hung run green.
4. `scripts/gpu-report.js`: `dist/node.js` is imported through a computed `file://` URL after an `existsSync` check (knip would otherwise report an unresolved import of a gitignored file, and the missing-build case gets exit 3 with a clear reason instead of a stack trace); `dawnFlags(options)` is printed to stderr so stdout stays the ONE JSON document; the device is requested with the four limits raised to the adapter's values, falling back to a default device from a FRESH adapter when that request is refused; the `nvidiaSmi` block also carries `minMemoryUsedMiB` (the "own process footprint estimate" bench-compare's quiet-GPU rule subtracts, contract 6.8); `features` is the full sorted `adapter.features` list (the contract's two-entry example is illustrative).
5. `parseGpuRequire`: the keywords `any` / `hardware` are matched after trim + lower-case (so `ANY` is the level, not a vendor named "any"); `raw` keeps the original text; a vendor is lower-cased as declared.
6. `scripts/bench-compare.js` P0 stub: option parsing (`--threshold <n>` validated positive, `--class <name>`), the two "nothing to compare" exits (exit 0) and exit 2 on an unknown option or a malformed input are in place; when BOTH inputs exist it prints what it found and exits 0 -- the 3x rule and the quiet-GPU skip are P1-T7's edit (contract 6.8). Nothing writes `benchmarks/out/` at P0, so that branch is unreachable until P1.
7. `benchmarks/run.ts` P0 stub: prints `no benchmark groups until P1: nothing timed, nothing written` (echoing any requested group names), exits 0 whatever the arguments (contract 2.9 delta 4).
8. Every JSON / TS / JS file is given in the form `prettier --write` (packages/.prettierrc) produces; package.json therefore uses the expanded json-stringify layout graph-io's has. The parsed content equals the contract's text (the manifest checker asserts every field).
9. `pnpm install` under the workspace's `.npmrc` (`auto-install-peers=true`) resolves and installs the OPTIONAL peers `@graphty/algorithms` (1.7.2 today) and `@graphty/layout` (1.6.2 today) into this package's importer -- verified with `pnpm install --lockfile-only` in a scratch copy of the workspace. The manifest stays as the contract gives it and `.npmrc` is not touched (it is not in this task's file list); the two packages are installed but unused, the ESLint CPU ban and P0-T3's layers test keep them out of `src/`, and G0.md records the fact (P0-T4).
10. `packages/knip.config.ts` header: "the two package entries below" becomes "the package entries below" (a one-word comment fix beside the fourth block).
11. The six checkers live in `/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/p0-t2/` (gitignored `tmp/`), load the package files through `file://` URLs or `createRequire(<package>/package.json)`, and are the task's tests; they are not part of the package.
12. `runnerClass(info, env)` defaults `env` to `process.env` as declared, but to an empty record where `process` does not exist, so the module can be imported by the browser bundle of P3-T6 (the bench bridge passes an explicit env there); the declared signature is unchanged.

Write `/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/p0-t2/check-policy.mjs` (hand-computed rows for `parseGpuRequire`, `isSoftwareInfo`, `checkAdapter`, `runnerClass`):

```js
/**
 * P0-T2 scratch check of scripts/gpu-policy.js and scripts/runner-class.js against hand-computed rows
 * (the vitest pin of the same rows is P0-T3's test/device/policy.test.ts). Run from anywhere:
 *   node tmp/p0-t2/check-policy.mjs
 * Exit 0 when every row matches, 1 otherwise (each mismatch printed).
 */
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

const PKG = process.env.P0_PKG_DIR ?? "/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms";
const policy = await import(pathToFileURL(`${PKG}/scripts/gpu-policy.js`).href);
const rc = await import(pathToFileURL(`${PKG}/scripts/runner-class.js`).href);

let failures = 0;
function eq(label, actual, expected) {
    const a = JSON.stringify(actual);
    const e = JSON.stringify(expected);
    if (a !== e) {
        failures++;
        console.error(`FAIL ${label}\n  actual:   ${a}\n  expected: ${e}`);
    }
}

// ---- parseGpuRequire
eq("parse unset", policy.parseGpuRequire(undefined), { level: "skip", vendor: null, raw: "" });
eq("parse empty", policy.parseGpuRequire(""), { level: "skip", vendor: null, raw: "" });
eq("parse any", policy.parseGpuRequire("any"), { level: "any", vendor: null, raw: "any" });
eq("parse hardware", policy.parseGpuRequire("hardware"), { level: "hardware", vendor: null, raw: "hardware" });
eq("parse nvidia", policy.parseGpuRequire("nvidia"), { level: "vendor", vendor: "nvidia", raw: "nvidia" });
eq("parse Intel (lower-cased vendor, raw kept)", policy.parseGpuRequire("Intel"), { level: "vendor", vendor: "intel", raw: "Intel" });

// ---- isSoftwareInfo (the 6-row table policy.test.ts compares with src isSoftwareAdapter)
const NVIDIA_NODE = { vendor: "nvidia", architecture: "lovelace", isFallbackAdapter: false };
const NVIDIA_BROWSER = { vendor: "nvidia", architecture: "ada-lovelace", isFallbackAdapter: false };
const LAVAPIPE = { vendor: "mesa", architecture: "software", isFallbackAdapter: true };
const SWIFTSHADER = { vendor: "google", architecture: "swiftshader", isFallbackAdapter: true };
const FALLBACK_ONLY = { vendor: "intel", architecture: "gen-12lp", isFallbackAdapter: true };
const NO_FLAG = { vendor: "apple", architecture: "common-3" };
eq("software nvidia node", policy.isSoftwareInfo(NVIDIA_NODE), false);
eq("software nvidia browser", policy.isSoftwareInfo(NVIDIA_BROWSER), false);
eq("software lavapipe", policy.isSoftwareInfo(LAVAPIPE), true);
eq("software swiftshader", policy.isSoftwareInfo(SWIFTSHADER), true);
eq("software fallback flag only", policy.isSoftwareInfo(FALLBACK_ONLY), true);
eq("software no flag", policy.isSoftwareInfo(NO_FLAG), false);

// ---- checkAdapter
const P = policy.parseGpuRequire;
eq("check absent skip", policy.checkAdapter(null, P(undefined)), { ok: false, skip: true, reason: "E_NO_ADAPTER: no WebGPU adapter (GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE unset: skipping)" });
eq("check absent any", policy.checkAdapter(null, P("any")), { ok: false, skip: false, reason: "E_NO_ADAPTER: no WebGPU adapter but GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=any requires one" });
eq("check lavapipe under skip", policy.checkAdapter(LAVAPIPE, P(undefined)), { ok: true, skip: false, reason: null });
eq("check lavapipe under any", policy.checkAdapter(LAVAPIPE, P("any")), { ok: true, skip: false, reason: null });
eq("check lavapipe under hardware", policy.checkAdapter(LAVAPIPE, P("hardware")), { ok: false, skip: false, reason: "E_SOFTWARE_ONLY: adapter mesa/software is a software adapter but GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=hardware" });
eq("check nvidia under hardware", policy.checkAdapter(NVIDIA_NODE, P("hardware")), { ok: true, skip: false, reason: null });
eq("check nvidia under nvidia", policy.checkAdapter(NVIDIA_NODE, P("nvidia")), { ok: true, skip: false, reason: null });
eq("check vendor mismatch", policy.checkAdapter(NVIDIA_NODE, P("intel")), { ok: false, skip: false, reason: 'E_NO_ADAPTER: adapter vendor "nvidia" (nvidia/lovelace) does not match GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=intel' });
eq("check browser fallback under vendor", policy.checkAdapter({ vendor: "nvidia", architecture: "ada-lovelace", isFallbackAdapter: true }, P("nvidia"), { browser: true }), { ok: false, skip: false, reason: "E_SOFTWARE_ONLY: adapter nvidia/ada-lovelace reports isFallbackAdapter=true in the browser under GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=nvidia" });
eq("check browser nvidia under vendor", policy.checkAdapter(NVIDIA_BROWSER, P("nvidia"), { browser: true }), { ok: true, skip: false, reason: null });
eq("check node ignores the fallback clause", policy.checkAdapter({ vendor: "nvidia", architecture: "lovelace" }, P("nvidia")), { ok: true, skip: false, reason: null });
eq("check frozen verdict", Object.isFrozen(policy.checkAdapter(NVIDIA_NODE, P("any"))), true);

// ---- runnerClass (the 4-row table of contract 5.5 plus the override)
const ENV_EMPTY = {};
eq("class nvidia dawn", rc.runnerClass({ vendor: "nvidia", architecture: "lovelace", description: "NVIDIA: 580.173.02 580.173.2.0" }, ENV_EMPTY), "nvidia-lovelace-driver580");
eq("class nvidia ada", rc.runnerClass({ vendor: "nvidia", architecture: "ada-lovelace", description: "NVIDIA: 580.173.02 580.173.2.0" }, ENV_EMPTY), "nvidia-ada-lovelace-driver580");
eq("class lavapipe", rc.runnerClass({ vendor: "mesa", architecture: "software", description: "llvmpipe: Mesa 23.2.1-1ubuntu3.1~22.04.3 (LLVM 15.0.7) 0.0.1" }, ENV_EMPTY), "mesa-software-driver23");
eq("class no digits", rc.runnerClass({ vendor: "google", architecture: "swiftshader", description: "SwiftShader device" }, ENV_EMPTY), "google-swiftshader-driver0");
eq("class vendor with a space", rc.runnerClass({ vendor: "Some Vendor", architecture: "Gen 12", description: "v7.1" }, ENV_EMPTY), "some_vendor-gen_12-driver7");
eq("class override verbatim", rc.runnerClass({ vendor: "nvidia", architecture: "turing", description: "NVIDIA: 535.1" }, { GRAPHTY_RUNNER_CLASS: "gpu-linux-t4" }), "gpu-linux-t4");
eq("class empty override ignored", rc.runnerClass({ vendor: "nvidia", architecture: "turing", description: "NVIDIA: 535.1" }, { GRAPHTY_RUNNER_CLASS: "" }), "nvidia-turing-driver535");

if (failures > 0) {
    console.error(`${failures} mismatch(es)`);
    process.exit(1);
}
console.log("check-policy: every row matches");
```

Write `/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/p0-t2/check-run-browser.mjs` (the wrapper's pure parts):

```js
/**
 * P0-T2 scratch check of scripts/run-browser-project.js's pure parts (readSummary, verdict) against hand-made
 * cases. Run: node tmp/p0-t2/check-run-browser.mjs -- exit 0 when every case matches, 1 otherwise.
 */
import { mkdtempSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

const PKG = process.env.P0_PKG_DIR ?? "/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms";
const m = await import(pathToFileURL(`${PKG}/scripts/run-browser-project.js`).href);

let failures = 0;
function eq(label, actual, expected) {
    const a = JSON.stringify(actual);
    const e = JSON.stringify(expected);
    if (a !== e) {
        failures++;
        console.error(`FAIL ${label}\n  actual:   ${a}\n  expected: ${e}`);
    }
}

const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "p0-t2-run-browser-"));
const good = join(dir, "good.json");
writeFileSync(good, JSON.stringify({ numTotalTests: 3, numPassedTests: 3, numFailedTests: 0, testResults: [] }));
const bad = join(dir, "bad.json");
writeFileSync(bad, JSON.stringify({ numTotalTests: 3, numPassedTests: 2, numFailedTests: 1 }));
const empty = join(dir, "empty.json");
writeFileSync(empty, JSON.stringify({ numTotalTests: 0, numPassedTests: 0, numFailedTests: 0 }));
const garbage = join(dir, "garbage.json");
writeFileSync(garbage, "{ not json");
const missing = join(dir, "missing.json");

eq("readSummary good", m.readSummary(good), { numTotalTests: 3, numPassedTests: 3, numFailedTests: 0 });
eq("readSummary garbage", m.readSummary(garbage), null);
eq("readSummary missing", m.readSummary(missing), null);

const S = (f) => m.readSummary(f);
eq("verdict 0", m.verdict(0, null).exitCode, 0);
eq("verdict 124 good", m.verdict(124, S(good)).exitCode, 0);
eq("verdict 137 good", m.verdict(137, S(good)).exitCode, 0);
eq("verdict 124 bad", m.verdict(124, S(bad)).exitCode, 1);
eq("verdict 124 empty", m.verdict(124, S(empty)).exitCode, 1);
eq("verdict 124 garbage", m.verdict(124, S(garbage)).exitCode, 1);
eq("verdict 124 missing", m.verdict(124, S(missing)).exitCode, 1);
eq("verdict 1 good passes through", m.verdict(1, S(good)).exitCode, 1);
eq("verdict 2 passes through", m.verdict(2, null).exitCode, 2);
eq("constants", [m.RESULTS_FILE, m.TIMEOUT_SECONDS, m.KILL_AFTER_SECONDS, m.EXIT_TIMED_OUT, m.EXIT_KILLED], ["browser-results.json", 600, 10, 124, 137]);
eq("hasGnuTimeout on this box", m.hasGnuTimeout(), true);

if (failures > 0) {
    console.error(`${failures} mismatch(es)`);
    process.exit(1);
}
console.log("check-run-browser: every case matches");
```

```js
/**
 * P0-T2 scratch check of scripts/gpu-report.js's pure helpers (parseSmiLine, summarizeSamples, median,
 * pickLimits, nodeGpuOptions) and its exit-3 path when dist/node.js is absent. The adapter path is exercised
 * by hand after P0-T3's build (the commands and expected documents are in the plan).
 * Run: node tmp/p0-t2/check-gpu-report.mjs -- exit 0 when every case matches, 1 otherwise.
 */
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

const PKG = process.env.P0_PKG_DIR ?? "/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms";
const m = await import(pathToFileURL(`${PKG}/scripts/gpu-report.js`).href);

let failures = 0;
function eq(label, actual, expected) {
    const a = JSON.stringify(actual);
    const e = JSON.stringify(expected);
    if (a !== e) {
        failures++;
        console.error(`FAIL ${label}\n  actual:   ${a}\n  expected: ${e}`);
    }
}

eq("parseSmiLine idle", m.parseSmiLine("0 %, 2 MiB"), { utilizationGpu: 0, memoryUsedMiB: 2 });
eq("parseSmiLine busy", m.parseSmiLine("37 %, 1024 MiB"), { utilizationGpu: 37, memoryUsedMiB: 1024 });
eq("parseSmiLine garbage", m.parseSmiLine("N/A, N/A"), null);
eq("parseSmiLine one field", m.parseSmiLine("12 %"), null);
eq("summarizeSamples", m.summarizeSamples([{ utilizationGpu: 2, memoryUsedMiB: 16 }, { utilizationGpu: 0, memoryUsedMiB: 20 }, { utilizationGpu: 5, memoryUsedMiB: 16 }]), {
    available: true,
    samples: [{ utilizationGpu: 2, memoryUsedMiB: 16 }, { utilizationGpu: 0, memoryUsedMiB: 20 }, { utilizationGpu: 5, memoryUsedMiB: 16 }],
    maxUtilization: 5,
    maxMemoryUsedMiB: 20,
    minMemoryUsedMiB: 16,
});
eq("summarizeSamples empty", m.summarizeSamples([]), { available: true, samples: [], maxUtilization: 0, maxMemoryUsedMiB: 0, minMemoryUsedMiB: 0 });
eq("median odd", m.median([3, 1, 2]), 2);
eq("median even", m.median([4, 1, 3, 2]), 2.5);
eq("median one", m.median([0.25]), 0.25);
eq("pickLimits", m.pickLimits({ maxBufferSize: 1, maxStorageBufferBindingSize: 2, maxStorageBuffersPerShaderStage: 3, maxComputeWorkgroupsPerDimension: 4, maxTextureDimension1D: 99 }), { maxBufferSize: 1, maxStorageBufferBindingSize: 2, maxStorageBuffersPerShaderStage: 3, maxComputeWorkgroupsPerDimension: 4 });
eq("LIMIT_NAMES", m.LIMIT_NAMES, ["maxBufferSize", "maxStorageBufferBindingSize", "maxStorageBuffersPerShaderStage", "maxComputeWorkgroupsPerDimension"]);
eq("nodeGpuOptions empty", m.nodeGpuOptions({}), {});
eq("nodeGpuOptions adapter", m.nodeGpuOptions({ GRAPHTY_GPU_ADAPTER: "llvmpipe" }), { adapter: "llvmpipe" });
eq("nodeGpuOptions features", m.nodeGpuOptions({ GRAPHTY_DAWN_FEATURES: "a, b,,c" }), { dawnFeatures: ["a", "b", "c"] });
eq("nodeGpuOptions empty strings ignored", m.nodeGpuOptions({ GRAPHTY_GPU_ADAPTER: "", GRAPHTY_DAWN_FEATURES: "" }), {});
eq("exit codes", [m.EXIT_OK, m.EXIT_UNEXPECTED, m.EXIT_POLICY, m.EXIT_NO_ADAPTER, m.ROUND_TRIPS, m.SMI_SECONDS], [0, 1, 2, 3, 10, 10]);

// the exit-3 path needs dist/node.js to be absent (before P0-T3's build); skipped -- not failed -- when dist exists
if (!existsSync(`${PKG}/dist/node.js`)) {
    const run = spawnSync(process.execPath, [`${PKG}/scripts/gpu-report.js`], { encoding: "utf8", env: { ...process.env, GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE: "any" } });
    eq("exit 3 without dist", run.status, 3);
    const doc = JSON.parse(run.stdout);
    eq("exit 3 document ok/adapter", [doc.ok, doc.adapter, doc.policy.level, doc.runnerClass], [false, null, "any", "unknown"]);
    eq("exit 3 reason names dist/node.js", doc.reason.startsWith("E_NO_WEBGPU: ") && doc.reason.includes("dist/node.js not found"), true);
} else {
    console.log("dist/node.js exists: the exit-3 case is skipped (run the adapter cases of the plan by hand)");
}

if (failures > 0) {
    console.error(`${failures} mismatch(es)`);
    process.exit(1);
}
console.log("check-gpu-report: every case matches");
```

```js
/**
 * P0-T2 scratch check of eslint.config.js: the computed config of representative (virtual) source paths carries
 * the layer zones, the types-zone `paths` + `patterns` pair, the runtime-global bans and the test relaxations
 * of contract 2.4 (P0-T3's test/layers.test.ts pins the same facts through ESLint.calculateConfigForFile).
 * Run: node tmp/p0-t2/check-eslint-zones.mjs -- exit 0 when every expectation holds, 1 otherwise.
 */
import { createRequire } from "node:module";

const PKG = process.env.P0_PKG_DIR ?? "/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms";
const require = createRequire(`${PKG}/package.json`);
const { ESLint } = require("eslint");
const eslint = new ESLint({ cwd: PKG });

let failures = 0;
function check(label, condition, detail) {
    if (!condition) {
        failures++;
        console.error(`FAIL ${label}${detail === undefined ? "" : `: ${JSON.stringify(detail)}`}`);
    }
}
function patternsOf(rule) {
    if (rule === undefined || rule[0] === "off" || rule[0] === 0) {
        return [];
    }
    const options = rule[1] ?? {};
    return (options.patterns ?? []).flatMap((p) => (typeof p === "string" ? [p] : p.group));
}
function pathsOf(rule) {
    if (rule === undefined || rule[0] === "off" || rule[0] === 0) {
        return [];
    }
    const options = rule[1] ?? {};
    return (options.paths ?? []).map((p) => (typeof p === "string" ? p : p.name));
}
function globalsOf(rule) {
    if (rule === undefined || rule[0] === "off" || rule[0] === 0) {
        return [];
    }
    return rule.slice(1).map((g) => (typeof g === "string" ? g : g.name));
}

const cfg = async (file) => (await eslint.calculateConfigForFile(file)).rules;

// ---- layer zones (core no-restricted-imports)
const memory = await cfg("src/memory/residency.ts");
check("memory zone forbids kernel", patternsOf(memory["no-restricted-imports"]).includes("../kernel/*"));
check("memory zone forbids context", patternsOf(memory["no-restricted-imports"]).includes("../context.js"));
check("memory zone allows device (not listed)", !patternsOf(memory["no-restricted-imports"]).includes("../device/*"));
const kernel = await cfg("src/kernel/kernel.ts");
check("kernel zone forbids registry", patternsOf(kernel["no-restricted-imports"]).includes("../kernels.js"));
check("kernel zone allows memory (type edges live in src/types/memory.ts)", !patternsOf(kernel["no-restricted-imports"]).includes("../memory/*"));
const registry = await cfg("src/kernels.ts");
check("kernels.ts forbids primitives", patternsOf(registry["no-restricted-imports"]).includes("./primitives/*"));
check("kernels.ts allows wgsl", !patternsOf(registry["no-restricted-imports"]).includes("./wgsl/*"));
const context = await cfg("src/context.ts");
check("context forbids registry", patternsOf(context["no-restricted-imports"]).includes("./kernels.js"));
check("context allows memory and kernel", !patternsOf(context["no-restricted-imports"]).some((p) => p.startsWith("./memory") || p.startsWith("./kernel/")));
const primitives = await cfg("src/primitives/reduce.ts");
check("primitives forbid wgsl and context", ["../wgsl/*", "../context.js"].every((p) => patternsOf(primitives["no-restricted-imports"]).includes(p)));
const layouts = await cfg("src/layouts/forceatlas2.ts");
check("layouts forbid algorithms and accelerator", ["../algorithms/*", "../accelerator.js"].every((p) => patternsOf(layouts["no-restricted-imports"]).includes(p)));
const entry = await cfg("src/node/index.ts");
check("node entry forbids the barrel and memory", ["../index.js", "../memory/*"].every((p) => patternsOf(entry["no-restricted-imports"]).includes(p)));
const barrel = await cfg("src/index.ts");
check("barrel forbids the entries", ["./browser/*", "./node/*"].every((p) => patternsOf(barrel["no-restricted-imports"]).includes(p)));

// ---- the types zone: ONE options object carrying both paths (CPU ban) and patterns (the zone)
const types = await cfg("src/types/context.ts");
const typesRule = types["@typescript-eslint/no-restricted-imports"];
check("types zone has the CPU paths", ["@graphty/algorithms", "@graphty/layout"].every((p) => pathsOf(typesRule).includes(p)), pathsOf(typesRule));
check("types zone has the patterns", patternsOf(typesRule).includes("../device/*") && patternsOf(typesRule).includes("../context.js"), patternsOf(typesRule));
check("types zone patterns allow type imports", (typesRule?.[1]?.patterns ?? []).every((p) => p.allowTypeImports === true));
check("types zone paths forbid type imports of the CPU packages", (typesRule?.[1]?.paths ?? []).every((p) => p.allowTypeImports !== true));
const accel = await cfg("src/types/accelerator.ts");
const accelRule = accel["@typescript-eslint/no-restricted-imports"];
check("accelerator types allow CPU type imports", (accelRule?.[1]?.paths ?? []).every((p) => p.allowTypeImports === true), accelRule?.[1]?.paths);
check("accelerator types keep the zone patterns", patternsOf(accelRule).includes("../context.js"));
// the generic CPU ban block must not clobber the types zone
const memoryTs = memory["@typescript-eslint/no-restricted-imports"];
check("memory carries the CPU ban (paths only)", pathsOf(memoryTs).includes("@graphty/algorithms") && patternsOf(memoryTs).length === 0, memoryTs);

// ---- runtime globals
const memGlobals = globalsOf(memory["no-restricted-globals"]);
check("core bans navigator, window, document, process", ["navigator", "window", "document", "process"].every((g) => memGlobals.includes(g)), memGlobals);
const browserEntry = await cfg("src/browser/index.ts");
const browserGlobals = globalsOf(browserEntry["no-restricted-globals"]);
check("browser entry may read navigator but not process", !browserGlobals.includes("navigator") && browserGlobals.includes("process"), browserGlobals);
const nodeGlobals = globalsOf(entry["no-restricted-globals"]);
check("node entry may read process but not navigator", nodeGlobals.includes("navigator") && !nodeGlobals.includes("process"), nodeGlobals);

// ---- tests are relaxed
const test = await cfg("test/device/acquire.test.ts");
check("tests: no-restricted-imports off", test["no-restricted-imports"]?.[0] === "off" || test["no-restricted-imports"]?.[0] === 0, test["no-restricted-imports"]);
check("tests: no-restricted-globals off", test["no-restricted-globals"]?.[0] === "off" || test["no-restricted-globals"]?.[0] === 0);
check("tests: root relaxation (no-console off)", test["no-console"]?.[0] === "off" || test["no-console"]?.[0] === 0);
// ---- the root config is spread first (its rules reach src/)
check("root: explicit return types on src", memory["@typescript-eslint/explicit-function-return-type"]?.[0] === "error" || memory["@typescript-eslint/explicit-function-return-type"]?.[0] === 2);
check("root: no-console allows warn/error only", JSON.stringify(memory["no-console"]?.[1]) === JSON.stringify({ allow: ["warn", "error"] }), memory["no-console"]);
// ---- ignored paths of the root config
check("scripts are ignored", (await eslint.isPathIgnored(`${PKG}/scripts/gpu-report.js`)) === true);
check("benchmarks are ignored", (await eslint.isPathIgnored(`${PKG}/benchmarks/run.ts`)) === true);
check("the config itself is ignored", (await eslint.isPathIgnored(`${PKG}/vitest.config.ts`)) === true);

if (failures > 0) {
    console.error(`${failures} expectation(s) failed`);
    process.exit(1);
}
console.log("check-eslint-zones: every expectation holds");
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""P0-T2 scratch check of .github/workflows/ci.yml and gpu.yml: both parse as YAML (PyYAML 6, the system
python3) and carry the facts of contract 2.9 plus its P0 deltas 1, 3 and 7. Run from the repository root:
    python3 tmp/p0-t2/check-workflows.py
Exit 0 when every expectation holds, 1 otherwise (each failure printed)."""
import os
import sys

import yaml

ROOT = os.environ.get("P0_REPO_ROOT", "/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms")
failures = 0


def check(label, condition, detail=None):
    global failures
    if not condition:
        failures += 1
        print(f"FAIL {label}" + ("" if detail is None else f": {detail!r}"))


def steps(job):
    return job["steps"]


def runs(job):
    return [s.get("run", "") for s in steps(job)]


ci = yaml.safe_load(open(os.path.join(ROOT, ".github/workflows/ci.yml")))
gpu = yaml.safe_load(open(os.path.join(ROOT, ".github/workflows/gpu.yml")))

# ---- ci.yml
check("ci name", ci["name"] == "CI")
check("ci jobs", list(ci["jobs"].keys()) == ["test"])
test = ci["jobs"]["test"]
check("ci runs-on", test["runs-on"] == "ubuntu-latest")
check("ci timeout", test["timeout-minutes"] == 30)
check("ci defaults", test["defaults"]["run"] == {"working-directory": "packages", "shell": "bash"})
check("ci permissions", ci["permissions"] == {"contents": "read"})
ci_runs = "\n".join(runs(test))
check("ci installs lavapipe", "mesa-vulkan-drivers libvulkan1" in ci_runs)
check("ci frozen lockfile", "pnpm install --frozen-lockfile" in ci_runs)
check("ci build lint knip", all(x in ci_runs for x in ["pnpm run build", "pnpm -r run lint", "pnpm exec knip"]))
node_step = next(s for s in steps(test) if "Node suite" in s.get("name", ""))
check("ci node step env", node_step["env"] == {
    "GRAPHTY_GPU_ADAPTER": "llvmpipe", "GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE": "any",
    "VK_DRIVER_FILES": "/usr/share/vulkan/icd.d/lvp_icd.x86_64.json", "XDG_RUNTIME_DIR": "/tmp"}, node_step.get("env"))
check("ci node step cwd", node_step["working-directory"] == "packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms")
check("ci coverage run", "pnpm exec vitest run --project=node --coverage" in node_step["run"])
check("ci delta 1: --passWithNoTests on the no-subgroups pass",
      "GRAPHTY_GPU_NO_SUBGROUPS=1 pnpm exec vitest run --project=node test/primitives test/layouts --passWithNoTests" in node_step["run"], node_step["run"])
browser_step = next(s for s in steps(test) if "Browser smoke" in s.get("name", ""))
check("ci browser env", browser_step["env"] == {"GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU": "swiftshader", "GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE": "any"})
check("ci browser wrapper", browser_step["run"].startswith("node scripts/run-browser-project.js"))
upload = next(s for s in steps(test) if s.get("uses", "").startswith("actions/upload-artifact"))
check("ci lcov artifact", upload["with"]["path"] == "packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/coverage/lcov.info" and upload["with"]["overwrite"] is True)
check("ci pnpm action reads packages/package.json", any(s.get("with", {}).get("package_json_file") == "packages/package.json" for s in steps(test)))

# ---- gpu.yml
check("gpu name", gpu["name"] == "GPU")
check("gpu jobs", list(gpu["jobs"].keys()) == ["changed", "test-gpu", "gpu-nightly-report"])
check("gpu concurrency group", gpu["concurrency"]["group"] == "gpu-lane-${{ github.event.pull_request.number || github.ref }}")
check("gpu concurrency cancel", gpu["concurrency"]["cancel-in-progress"] == "${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request' }}")
changed = gpu["jobs"]["changed"]
check("gpu changed outputs", changed["outputs"] == {"run": "${{ steps.check.outputs.run }}"})
tg = gpu["jobs"]["test-gpu"]
check("gpu runs-on", tg["runs-on"] == "gpu-linux-t4")
check("gpu timeout", tg["timeout-minutes"] == 45)
check("gpu needs", tg["needs"] == "changed")
check("gpu if guards label and same repo", "contains(github.event.pull_request.labels.*.name, 'gpu')" in tg["if"] and "github.event.pull_request.head.repo.full_name == github.repository" in tg["if"])
check("gpu delta 7: env", tg["env"] == {"GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE": "nvidia", "GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU": "nvidia", "GRAPHTY_RUNNER_CLASS": "gpu-linux-t4", "XDG_RUNTIME_DIR": "/tmp"}, tg.get("env"))
check("gpu defaults", tg["defaults"]["run"] == {"working-directory": "packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms", "shell": "bash"})
gpu_runs = runs(tg)
report = next(r for r in gpu_runs if "gpu-report.js" in r)
check("gpu report step has no tee", "tee" not in report and "node scripts/gpu-report.js > gpu-report.json" in report)
canary = next(r for r in gpu_runs if "gpu-upload.test.ts" in r)
check("gpu canary greps the vendor line", 'grep -q "adapter vendor=nvidia" canary.log' in canary)
check("gpu node + limits", "pnpm exec vitest run --project=node --project=node-limits" in gpu_runs)
check("gpu no-subgroups whole project", any(r.startswith("GRAPHTY_GPU_NO_SUBGROUPS=1 pnpm exec vitest run --project=node") for r in gpu_runs))
check("gpu bench + compare", "pnpm run bench" in gpu_runs and "node scripts/bench-compare.js" in gpu_runs)
raw = open(os.path.join(ROOT, ".github/workflows/gpu.yml")).read()
check("gpu delta 3: results/ not baselines/", "benchmarks/results/gpu-linux-t4.json" in raw and "benchmarks/baselines" not in raw)
upload = next(s for s in steps(tg) if s.get("uses", "").startswith("actions/upload-artifact"))
check("gpu artifact", upload["with"]["retention-days"] == 90 and "gpu-report.json" in upload["with"]["path"] and "benchmarks/out/" in upload["with"]["path"])
nightly = gpu["jobs"]["gpu-nightly-report"]
check("gpu nightly permissions", nightly["permissions"] == {"issues": "write"} and nightly["runs-on"] == "ubuntu-latest")
check("gpu workflow permissions read-only", gpu["permissions"] == {"contents": "read"})
for f in ["ci.yml", "gpu.yml"]:
    data = open(os.path.join(ROOT, ".github/workflows", f), "rb").read()
    check(f"{f} is ASCII", all(b < 128 for b in data))

if failures:
    print(f"{failures} expectation(s) failed")
    sys.exit(1)
print("check-workflows: every expectation holds")
```

```js
/**
 * P0-T2 scratch check of the manifest and config files against contract 2.1-2.3, 2.6 and 2.8: package.json
 * (name, exports == ENTRIES, the pins, the scripts), project.json, the tsconfig trio, scripts/entries.js and
 * the four workspace-root edits. P0-T3's test/build-output.test.ts pins the manifest facts for good; this
 * check exists so P0-T2 can verify its own files before any test file exists.
 * Run: node tmp/p0-t2/check-manifest.mjs -- exit 0 when every expectation holds, 1 otherwise.
 */
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

const PKG = process.env.P0_PKG_DIR ?? "/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms";
const WS = process.env.P0_WS_DIR ?? "/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages";
const json = (file) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(file, "utf8"));
const text = (file) => readFileSync(file, "utf8");

let failures = 0;
function check(label, condition, detail) {
    if (!condition) {
        failures++;
        console.error(`FAIL ${label}${detail === undefined ? "" : `: ${JSON.stringify(detail)}`}`);
    }
}

// ---- package.json (contract 2.1)
const pkg = json(`${PKG}/package.json`);
const { ENTRIES, declarationSpecifier } = await import(pathToFileURL(`${PKG}/scripts/entries.js`).href);
check("name", pkg.name === "@graphty/webgpu-graph-algorithms");
check("version 0.1.0 (the 0.0.0 placeholder replaced)", pkg.version === "0.1.0");
check("type module", pkg.type === "module");
check("main / types", pkg.main === "dist/webgpu-graph-algorithms.js" && pkg.types === "dist/webgpu-graph-algorithms.d.ts");
check("sideEffects false", pkg.sideEffects === false);
const exportKeys = Object.keys(pkg.exports);
const entryKeys = Object.keys(ENTRIES).map((k) => (k === "webgpu-graph-algorithms" ? "." : `./${k}`));
check("exports keys == ENTRIES", JSON.stringify(exportKeys) === JSON.stringify(entryKeys), { exportKeys, entryKeys });
for (const key of exportKeys) {
    const entry = pkg.exports[key];
    const name = key === "." ? "webgpu-graph-algorithms" : key.slice(2);
    check(`exports[${key}] types condition first`, Object.keys(entry)[0] === "types");
    check(`exports[${key}] targets dist/${name}`, entry.types === `./dist/${name}.d.ts` && entry.import === `./dist/${name}.js` && entry.default === `./dist/${name}.js`);
    check(`exports[${key}] has no require`, entry.require === undefined);
}
check("ENTRIES sources", JSON.stringify(ENTRIES) === JSON.stringify({ "webgpu-graph-algorithms": "src/index.ts", browser: "src/browser/index.ts", node: "src/node/index.ts" }));
check("declarationSpecifier", declarationSpecifier("src/node/index.ts") === "./src/node/index.js");
check("files", JSON.stringify(pkg.files) === JSON.stringify(["dist/", "src/", "README.md", "LICENSE"]));
check("publishConfig", JSON.stringify(pkg.publishConfig) === JSON.stringify({ access: "public", provenance: true }));
check("engines", pkg.engines.node === ">=18.19.0");
check("dependencies", JSON.stringify(pkg.dependencies) === JSON.stringify({ "@graphty/graph-format": "workspace:^", "@webgpu/types": "^0.1.72" }), pkg.dependencies);
check("peerDependencies", JSON.stringify(pkg.peerDependencies) === JSON.stringify({ "@graphty/algorithms": "^1.0.0", "@graphty/graph-format": "^0.1.0", "@graphty/layout": "^1.0.0", webgpu: ">=0.4.0 <1.0.0" }), pkg.peerDependencies);
check("optional peers", ["@graphty/algorithms", "@graphty/layout", "webgpu"].every((p) => pkg.peerDependenciesMeta[p]?.optional === true));
check("webgpu exact pin 0.4.0", pkg.devDependencies.webgpu === "0.4.0");
check("@vitest/browser and playwright are devDependencies", pkg.devDependencies["@vitest/browser"] === "^3.2.4" && pkg.devDependencies.playwright === "^1.54.1");
check("no CPU packages in devDependencies before W1", pkg.devDependencies["@graphty/algorithms"] === undefined && pkg.devDependencies["@graphty/layout"] === undefined);
const scripts = {
    build: "tsc -p tsconfig.build.json",
    "build:bundle": "node scripts/build-bundle.js",
    "build:all": "npm run build && npm run build:bundle",
    lint: "eslint && tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json && tsc -p tsconfig.strict-consumer.json",
    "lint:fix": "eslint --fix",
    typecheck: "tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json",
    "typecheck:strict-consumer": "tsc -p tsconfig.strict-consumer.json",
    test: "vitest",
    "test:ui": "vitest --ui",
    "test:run": "vitest run --project=node",
    "test:node": "vitest run --project=node",
    "test:browser": "vitest run --project=browser",
    "test:browser:ci": "node scripts/run-browser-project.js",
    "test:limits": "vitest run --project=node-limits",
    coverage: "vitest run --project=node --coverage",
    "coverage:preview": "npx serve coverage -p 9058",
    bench: "tsx benchmarks/run.ts",
    "bench:compare": "node scripts/bench-compare.js",
    benchmark: "tsx benchmarks/run.ts",
    "gpu:report": "node scripts/gpu-report.js",
    "ready:commit": "npm run build:all && npm run lint && npm run test:node",
};
check("scripts exactly", JSON.stringify(pkg.scripts) === JSON.stringify(scripts), pkg.scripts);
check("repository", pkg.repository.url === "git+https://github.com/graphty-org/webgpu-graph-algorithms.git" && pkg.repository.directory === "packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms");
check("no private flag, no browserslist", pkg.private === undefined && pkg.browserslist === undefined);

// ---- project.json (contract 2.2)
const project = json(`${PKG}/project.json`);
check("project name", project.name === "webgpu-graph-algorithms" && project.projectType === "library");
check("project targets", JSON.stringify(Object.keys(project.targets)) === JSON.stringify(["build", "test", "test:node", "test:browser", "test:limits", "test:ui", "coverage", "lint", "typecheck", "benchmark"]), Object.keys(project.targets));
check("project cwd is the monorepo path", Object.values(project.targets).every((t) => t.options.cwd === "webgpu-graph-algorithms"));
check("project test:browser uses the wrapper", project.targets["test:browser"].options.command === "npm run test:browser:ci");

// ---- the tsconfig trio (contract 2.3)
const tsconfig = json(`${PKG}/tsconfig.json`);
check("tsconfig extends the base", tsconfig.extends === "../tsconfig.base.json");
check("tsconfig types", JSON.stringify(tsconfig.compilerOptions.types) === JSON.stringify(["node", "vitest/globals", "vite/client", "@webgpu/types"]));
check("tsconfig strict flags off (design 14.5)", tsconfig.compilerOptions.noUncheckedIndexedAccess === false && tsconfig.compilerOptions.exactOptionalPropertyTypes === false);
check("tsconfig paths", JSON.stringify(tsconfig.compilerOptions.paths) === JSON.stringify({ "@graphty/webgpu-graph-algorithms": ["./src/index.ts"], "@graphty/webgpu-graph-algorithms/browser": ["./src/browser/index.ts"], "@graphty/webgpu-graph-algorithms/node": ["./src/node/index.ts"], "@graphty/graph-format": ["../graph-format/src/index.ts"] }));
check("tsconfig include", JSON.stringify(tsconfig.include) === JSON.stringify(["src/**/*.ts", "test/**/*.ts", "benchmarks/**/*.ts", "scripts/**/*.d.ts", "../graph-format/src/**/*.ts"]));
const build = json(`${PKG}/tsconfig.build.json`);
check("build stripInternal", build.compilerOptions.stripInternal === true && build.compilerOptions.noEmit === false && build.compilerOptions.rootDir === "." && build.compilerOptions.outDir === "./dist");
check("build include src only", JSON.stringify(build.include) === JSON.stringify(["src/**/*.ts"]) && build.exclude.includes("scripts"));
check("build types", JSON.stringify(build.compilerOptions.types) === JSON.stringify(["node", "@webgpu/types"]) && JSON.stringify(build.compilerOptions.paths) === "{}");
const strict = json(`${PKG}/tsconfig.strict-consumer.json`);
check("strict-consumer flags", strict.compilerOptions.noUncheckedIndexedAccess === true && strict.compilerOptions.exactOptionalPropertyTypes === true && strict.compilerOptions.composite === false);
check("strict-consumer paths to dist", strict.compilerOptions.paths["@graphty/webgpu-graph-algorithms"][0] === "./dist/webgpu-graph-algorithms.d.ts" && strict.compilerOptions.paths["@graphty/webgpu-graph-algorithms/*"][0] === "./dist/*.d.ts" && strict.compilerOptions.paths["@graphty/graph-format"][0] === "../graph-format/dist/graph-format.d.ts");
check("strict-consumer include", JSON.stringify(strict.include) === JSON.stringify(["test/types/**/*.test-d.ts"]));

// ---- the workspace-root edits (contract 2.8)
const workspace = text(`${WS}/pnpm-workspace.yaml`);
check("pnpm-workspace.yaml lists the package last", workspace === 'packages:\n    - "graph-format"\n    - "graph-io"\n    - "webgpu-graph-algorithms"\n', workspace);
const knip = text(`${WS}/knip.config.ts`);
check("knip workspace block", knip.includes('"webgpu-graph-algorithms": {') && knip.includes('ignoreDependencies: ["@vitest/browser", "playwright"],') && knip.includes('"benchmarks/layout-run.ts",') && knip.includes('"src/browser/index.ts",') && knip.includes('"src/node/index.ts",'));
check("knip block is after graph-io", knip.indexOf('"graph-io": {') < knip.indexOf('"webgpu-graph-algorithms": {'));
const wsPkg = json(`${WS}/package.json`);
check("workspace description", wsPkg.description === "Staging workspace for packages destined for the graphty monorepo (graph-format, graph-io, webgpu-graph-algorithms)", wsPkg.description);
const gitignore = text(`${WS}/.gitignore`);
check("workspace .gitignore", ["benchmarks/out/", "browser-results.json", "gpu-report.json"].every((line) => gitignore.split("\n").includes(line)));

// ---- every P0-T2 file exists
for (const f of ["package.json", "project.json", "tsconfig.json", "tsconfig.build.json", "tsconfig.strict-consumer.json", "eslint.config.js", "vitest.config.ts", "scripts/entries.js", "scripts/build-bundle.js", "scripts/bundle-types.js", "scripts/gpu-policy.js", "scripts/gpu-policy.d.ts", "scripts/runner-class.js", "scripts/runner-class.d.ts", "scripts/gpu-report.js", "scripts/run-browser-project.js", "scripts/bench-compare.js", "benchmarks/run.ts", "LICENSE"]) {
    check(`exists ${f}`, existsSync(`${PKG}/${f}`));
}
for (const f of [`${PKG}/package.json`, `${PKG}/project.json`, `${PKG}/tsconfig.json`, `${PKG}/tsconfig.build.json`, `${PKG}/tsconfig.strict-consumer.json`, `${PKG}/eslint.config.js`, `${PKG}/vitest.config.ts`, `${PKG}/scripts/entries.js`, `${PKG}/scripts/build-bundle.js`, `${PKG}/scripts/bundle-types.js`, `${PKG}/scripts/gpu-policy.js`, `${PKG}/scripts/gpu-policy.d.ts`, `${PKG}/scripts/runner-class.js`, `${PKG}/scripts/runner-class.d.ts`, `${PKG}/scripts/gpu-report.js`, `${PKG}/scripts/run-browser-project.js`, `${PKG}/scripts/bench-compare.js`, `${PKG}/benchmarks/run.ts`, `${WS}/knip.config.ts`, `${WS}/pnpm-workspace.yaml`, `${WS}/.gitignore`, `${WS}/package.json`]) {
    if (existsSync(f)) {
        const bytes = readFileSync(f);
        check(`ASCII ${f}`, bytes.every((b) => b < 128));
    }
}

if (failures > 0) {
    console.error(`${failures} expectation(s) failed`);
    process.exit(1);
}
console.log("check-manifest: every expectation holds");
```

Why these values (contract 2.1 notes): `workspace:^` (pnpm rewrites it to a caret range at publish, spec 2.5 / Q-31); `webgpu` devDependency is the EXACT pin `0.4.0` (0.6.1 needs glibc 2.38; the dev box has 2.35) while the peer range admits 0.6.x; the two CPU packages are optional peers only (D27); `test:run` is the node project so `pnpm -r run test:run` from the workspace root never launches Chromium; `repository.url` / `directory` keep the placeholder's values because npm provenance checks the publishing repository; the `lint` script carries the strict-consumer compile (spec 3.1).

```js
/**
 * The bundle entries of @graphty/webgpu-graph-algorithms (spec 2.5): the root barrel and the two acquisition
 * subpaths. Shared by scripts/build-bundle.js (dist/<name>.js) and scripts/bundle-types.js (dist/<name>.d.ts);
 * package.json "exports" lists the same names, which test/build-output.test.ts checks. There is NO root

 *
 * Keys are the output names under dist/, values the source entry relative to the package root.
 */

    "webgpu-graph-algorithms": "src/index.ts",
    browser: "src/browser/index.ts",
    node: "src/node/index.ts",
});

 * The declaration file a bundle entry re-exports: the tsc output of its source entry under dist/src/.
 * @param source - the source entry relative to the package root (`src/node/index.ts`)
 * @returns the relative import specifier for the shim (`./src/node/index.js`)
```

Write `/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/scripts/build-bundle.js` -- graph-io's `scripts/build-bundle.js` with only the header rewritten (contract 2.6: "byte-for-byte copies ... with the header comments rewritten"; `externalDependencies()` already externalises every `dependencies` + `peerDependencies` name, which covers `@graphty/graph-format`, `@webgpu/types`, `webgpu`, `@graphty/algorithms` and `@graphty/layout`):

```js
/**
 * Build Bundle Script
 *
 * One multi-entry vite library build (ES modules only) that emits, under dist/:
 * - webgpu-graph-algorithms.js, the root entry package.json "exports" ["."] points at (the core: GpuContext,
 *   the kernels, the algorithms and the layouts)
 * - browser.js, the "./browser" subpath (navigator.gpu acquisition, spec 2.3)
 * - node.js, the "./node" subpath -- the ONLY bundle that names the "webgpu" (Dawn) module, and only inside a
 *   dynamic import(), so a browser bundle never resolves it (spec 2.5; test/build-output.test.ts)
 * - chunks/*.js, the code two or more entries share, emitted once
 *
 * Runtime dependencies stay external so consumers install exactly one copy of each: every name under
 * package.json "dependencies" and "peerDependencies" (@graphty/graph-format, @webgpu/types, webgpu,
 * @graphty/algorithms, @graphty/layout), subpaths included. Run "npm run build" (tsc -p tsconfig.build.json)
 * first so that dist/src/ exists; scripts/bundle-types.js then writes one d.ts shim per entry that re-exports
 * the per-module declarations under dist/src/. At P0 the browser entry is `export {};`, so vite reports
 * "Generated an empty chunk: browser" -- a warning, not an error (contract 2.6).
 *
 * Copied from packages/graph-io/scripts/build-bundle.js; the code is unchanged, only this header differs.
 */

import { spawnSync } from "child_process";
import { readFileSync } from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "url";
import { build } from "vite";

import { ENTRIES } from "./entries.js";

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);
const packageRoot = path.resolve(__dirname, "..");

/**
 * Every package listed in dependencies or peerDependencies is external to the
 * bundle, including its subpaths.
 */
function externalDependencies() {
    const packageJson = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.resolve(packageRoot, "package.json"), "utf8"));
    const names = new Set([
        ...Object.keys(packageJson.dependencies ?? {}),
        ...Object.keys(packageJson.peerDependencies ?? {}),
    ]);
    return (id) => {
        for (const name of names) {
            if (id === name || id.startsWith(`${name}/`)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    };
}

async function buildBundle() {
    try {
        const entry = Object.fromEntries(
            Object.entries(ENTRIES).map(([name, source]) => [name, path.resolve(packageRoot, source)]),
        );
        await build({
            configFile: false,
            logLevel: "warn",
            build: {
                lib: {
                    entry,
                    formats: ["es"],
                    fileName: (_format, entryName) => `${entryName}.js`,
                },
                outDir: path.resolve(packageRoot, "dist"),
                emptyOutDir: false, // Keep the tsc output under dist/src/
                rollupOptions: {
                    external: externalDependencies(),
                    output: {
                        preserveModules: false,
                        chunkFileNames: "chunks/[name]-[hash].js",
                    },
                },
                minify: false,
                sourcemap: true,
            },
        });

        console.log(`Successfully built dist/{${Object.keys(ENTRIES).join(",")}}.js`);

        const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, [path.resolve(__dirname, "bundle-types.js")], {
            stdio: "inherit",
        });

        if (result.error) {
            throw result.error;
        }

        if (result.status !== 0) {
            throw new Error(`bundle-types.js exited with status ${String(result.status)}`);
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Error building bundle:", error);
        process.exit(1);
    }
}

buildBundle();
```

Write `/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/scripts/bundle-types.js` -- graph-io's `scripts/bundle-types.js` with only the header rewritten:

```js
/**
 * Bundle TypeScript declarations
 *
 * Writes one declaration shim per bundle entry: dist/webgpu-graph-algorithms.d.ts for the root (what
 * package.json "types" and exports["."] point at), dist/browser.d.ts and dist/node.d.ts for the two subpaths.
 * Each shim is a single re-export of the tsc output of its source entry under dist/src/ (the only barrels),
 * so a shim can never drift from the source export list. Every source entry starts with
 * `/// <reference types="@webgpu/types" />`, so the d.ts under dist/src/ resolve the GPU* names for consumers
 * (contract 2.3). tsconfig.strict-consumer.json compiles test/types/*.test-d.ts against these shims.
 *
 * Copied from packages/graph-io/scripts/bundle-types.js; the code is unchanged, only this header differs.
 */

import { existsSync, writeFileSync } from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "url";

import { declarationSpecifier, ENTRIES } from "./entries.js";

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);
const distDir = path.resolve(__dirname, "../dist");

function bundleTypes() {
    for (const [name, source] of Object.entries(ENTRIES)) {
        const declaration = path.resolve(distDir, source.replace(/\.ts$/, ".d.ts"));
        if (!existsSync(declaration)) {
            console.error(
                `Error: ${path.relative(distDir, declaration)} not found under dist/. Run "npm run build" first.`,
            );
            process.exit(1);
        }
        try {
            const content = `export * from "${declarationSpecifier(source)}";\n`;
            writeFileSync(path.resolve(distDir, `${name}.d.ts`), content, "utf8");
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(`Error writing dist/${name}.d.ts:`, error);
            process.exit(1);
        }
    }
    console.log(`Successfully created dist/{${Object.keys(ENTRIES).join(",")}}.d.ts`);
}

bundleTypes();
```

```ts
// scripts/gpu-policy.d.ts (the .js implements exactly this)
/** The parsed GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE. */
export interface GpuPolicy {
    readonly level: "skip" | "any" | "hardware" | "vendor";
    readonly vendor: string | null;
    readonly raw: string;
}
/** unset / "" -> skip; "any"; "hardware"; any other string -> vendor (lower-cased). */
export function parseGpuRequire(value: string | undefined): GpuPolicy;
/** architecture "software" | "swiftshader" or isFallbackAdapter === true (a copy of src isSoftwareAdapter; test/device/policy.test.ts keeps them equal). */
export function isSoftwareInfo(info: {
    readonly vendor: string;
    readonly architecture: string;
    readonly isFallbackAdapter?: boolean | undefined;
}): boolean;
/** The policy verdict for an adapter (null info = no adapter): skip -> { ok: false, reason, skip: true } when absent; any -> ok iff present; hardware -> ok iff !software; vendor -> ok iff vendor === policy.vendor and (browser ? isFallbackAdapter === false : true). */
export function checkAdapter(
    info: {
        readonly vendor: string;
        readonly architecture: string;
        readonly isFallbackAdapter?: boolean | undefined;
    } | null,
    policy: GpuPolicy,
    options?: { readonly browser?: boolean | undefined } | undefined,
): { readonly ok: boolean; readonly skip: boolean; readonly reason: string | null };
```

Write `/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/scripts/gpu-policy.js`:

```js
/**
 * The ONE copy of the adapter policy (spec 2.3, 11.2, D19; contract 6.5): GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE parsed by
 * parseGpuRequire(), the software test isSoftwareInfo() (a copy of src/device/acquire.ts isSoftwareAdapter,
 * kept equal by test/device/policy.test.ts) and the verdict checkAdapter(). Imported by test/setup/gpu.ts,
 * test/setup/browser.ts (through vitest.config.ts's forwarding) and scripts/gpu-report.js, so the vendor-match
 * rule has one copy. Plain ESM JavaScript (package "type": "module"); the declarations live in gpu-policy.d.ts.
 *
 * Policy levels:
 *   unset / ""       -> "skip"     (no adapter -> the test skips with the reason; a present adapter always passes)
 *   "any"            -> "any"      (an adapter must exist; lavapipe / SwiftShader count)
 *   "hardware"       -> "hardware" (additionally !isSoftwareInfo(info))
 *   any other string -> "vendor"   (additionally info.vendor === value, lower-cased; in the browser also
 *                                   isFallbackAdapter === false)
 */

/**
 * Parse GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE.
 * @param {string | undefined} value - the raw environment value
 * @returns {{ level: "skip" | "any" | "hardware" | "vendor", vendor: string | null, raw: string }} the policy
 */
export function parseGpuRequire(value) {
    const raw = value ?? "";
    const normalized = raw.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (normalized === "") {
        return Object.freeze({ level: "skip", vendor: null, raw });
    }
    if (normalized === "any") {
        return Object.freeze({ level: "any", vendor: null, raw });
    }
    if (normalized === "hardware") {
        return Object.freeze({ level: "hardware", vendor: null, raw });
    }
    return Object.freeze({ level: "vendor", vendor: normalized, raw });
}

/**
 * Software adapter test: architecture "software" (Dawn on Mesa llvmpipe) or "swiftshader" (Chromium's CPU
 * Vulkan), or isFallbackAdapter === true (Chromium marks SwiftShader; Dawn marks llvmpipe).
 * @param {{ vendor: string, architecture: string, isFallbackAdapter?: boolean | undefined }} info - adapter info
 * @returns {boolean} true for a software adapter
 */
export function isSoftwareInfo(info) {
    return info.architecture === "software" || info.architecture === "swiftshader" || info.isFallbackAdapter === true;
}

/**
 * The policy verdict for an adapter (null = no adapter).
 * @param {{ vendor: string, architecture: string, isFallbackAdapter?: boolean | undefined } | null} info - adapter info
 * @param {{ level: "skip" | "any" | "hardware" | "vendor", vendor: string | null, raw: string }} policy - parsed policy
 * @param {{ browser?: boolean | undefined } | undefined} [options] - browser: apply the isFallbackAdapter clause
 * @returns {{ ok: boolean, skip: boolean, reason: string | null }} the verdict
 */
export function checkAdapter(info, policy, options) {
    const browser = options?.browser === true;
    if (info === null) {
        if (policy.level === "skip") {
            return Object.freeze({
                ok: false,
                skip: true,
                reason: "E_NO_ADAPTER: no WebGPU adapter (GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE unset: skipping)",
            });
        }
        return Object.freeze({
            ok: false,
            skip: false,
            reason: `E_NO_ADAPTER: no WebGPU adapter but GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=${policy.raw} requires one`,
        });
    }
    const label = `${info.vendor}/${info.architecture}`;
    switch (policy.level) {
        case "skip":
        case "any":
            return Object.freeze({ ok: true, skip: false, reason: null });
        case "hardware":
            if (isSoftwareInfo(info)) {
                return Object.freeze({
                    ok: false,
                    skip: false,
                    reason: `E_SOFTWARE_ONLY: adapter ${label} is a software adapter but GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=hardware`,
                });
            }
            return Object.freeze({ ok: true, skip: false, reason: null });
        case "vendor":
            if (info.vendor !== policy.vendor) {
                return Object.freeze({
                    ok: false,
                    skip: false,
                    reason: `E_NO_ADAPTER: adapter vendor "${info.vendor}" (${label}) does not match GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=${policy.raw}`,
                });
            }
            if (browser && info.isFallbackAdapter !== false) {
                return Object.freeze({
                    ok: false,
                    skip: false,
                    reason: `E_SOFTWARE_ONLY: adapter ${label} reports isFallbackAdapter=${String(info.isFallbackAdapter)} in the browser under GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=${policy.raw}`,
                });
            }
            return Object.freeze({ ok: true, skip: false, reason: null });
        default:
            return Object.freeze({
                ok: false,
                skip: false,
                reason: `E_INVALID_ARGUMENT: unknown policy level ${String(policy.level)}`,
            });
    }
}
```

CONTRACT DECISION: `major` = the first run of digits in `description`, "0" when none (Dawn's NVIDIA description carries the driver version; SwiftShader / lavapipe carry Mesa's); every character outside [A-Za-z0-9_.-] becomes "_"; lower-cased. */

Write `/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/scripts/runner-class.js`:

```js
/**
 * The ONE copy of the benchmark runner-class rule (spec 10.4, 11.7; contract 6.4, 6.9): the name of the
 * benchmarks/results/<runner-class>.json baseline and of the benchmarks/out/<runner-class>.json run output.
 * scripts/gpu-report.js imports it, benchmarks/harness.ts re-exports it and test/device/policy.test.ts pins
 * it, so the three file names cannot drift. Plain ESM JavaScript; the declarations live in runner-class.d.ts.
 *
 * Rule: env.GRAPHTY_RUNNER_CLASS when set and non-empty (the T4 lane fixes "gpu-linux-t4" in gpu.yml so the
 * nightly file name never follows the partner image's driver), else "<vendor>-<architecture>-driver<major>"
 * where major is the FIRST run of digits in info.description ("0" when it has none; Dawn's NVIDIA description
 * is "NVIDIA: 580.173.02 580.173.2.0", lavapipe's "llvmpipe: Mesa 23.2.1-... (LLVM 15.0.7) 0.0.1"), every
 * character outside [A-Za-z0-9_.-] replaced by "_", lower-cased.
 */

/**
 * The runner class of an adapter.
 * @param {{ vendor: string, architecture: string, description: string }} info - the adapter facts
 * @param {Readonly<Record<string, string | undefined>> | undefined} [env] - the environment (default process.env;
 * an empty record where `process` does not exist, so the browser bundle of P3-T6 can import the module)
 * @returns {string} the runner class
 */
export function runnerClass(info, env) {
    const environment = env ?? (typeof process === "undefined" ? {} : process.env);
    const override = environment.GRAPHTY_RUNNER_CLASS;
    if (override !== undefined && override !== "") {
        return override;
    }
    const digits = /\d+/.exec(info.description);
    const major = digits === null ? "0" : digits[0];
    return `${info.vendor}-${info.architecture}-driver${major}`.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]/g, "_").toLowerCase();
}
```
