- [ ] **Step 19: Draft tmp/commit-p0.sh (the owner runs it at P0-T4; never run it yourself)**

`REPO/tmp/commit-p0.sh` (modelled on `REPO/tmp/commit-initial.sh`: explicit `git add` paths, `-A` where
the triage deleted or moved tracked files, GPG-signed commits, no `Co-Authored-By` and no `Claude-Session`
trailers, ever):

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# P0 commits of webgpu-graph-algorithms (spec 13 row P0; contract 7.1): the
# scaffold triage, the moved docs and the plans, the package's manifest /
# configs / scripts / CI lanes, the source and test skeleton, and -- after the
# first GitHub runs -- the G0 record.
#
#   bash tmp/commit-p0.sh            # stage and make the four skeleton commits
#   bash tmp/commit-p0.sh --dry-run  # stage and show what would happen
#   bash tmp/commit-p0.sh --gate     # after P0-T4: the fifth commit (G0.md, CLAUDE.md facts)
#
# Run it only after the P0 green check passed from packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms:
#   pnpm run build:all && pnpm run lint && pnpm exec vitest run --project=node
#   (cd .. && pnpm exec knip)
# The only prompt you should see is your commit signing passphrase, once per
# commit. The staging repository has no hooks of its own; the commits run with
# an empty hooks directory and HUSKY=0 (the scaffold's .husky is gone at P0).
# Commit messages carry no Co-Authored-By and no Claude-Session trailer.

set -euo pipefail

REPO="/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms"
PKG="packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms"
HOOKS="$REPO/tmp/githooks"
DRY_RUN=0
GATE=0
case "${1:-}" in
  --dry-run) DRY_RUN=1 ;;
  --gate) GATE=1 ;;
  "") ;;
  *) echo "usage: bash tmp/commit-p0.sh [--dry-run | --gate]"; exit 2 ;;
esac

cd "$REPO"

# The root scaffold P0-T1 retired (contract 1.1): tracked paths whose deletion
# is staged with `git add -A -- <path>`; README.md and .gitignore are rewrites.
SCAFFOLD_GONE=(
  .env.example .github/workflows/test.yml .vscode eslint.config.js examples
  knip.json package-lock.json package.json src test tsconfig.json
  vite.config.ts vitest.config.ts STRATEGY.md IMPLEMENTATION_CHECKLIST.md
)
ROOT_REWRITTEN=( README.md .gitignore )

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# An empty hooks directory (nothing to run; nothing skipped).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
setup_hooks() { mkdir -p "$HOOKS"; }
cleanup_hooks() { rm -rf "$HOOKS"; }
trap cleanup_hooks EXIT
export HUSKY=0

# Let gpg-agent find the terminal so pinentry can prompt here.
if tty -s; then export GPG_TTY="$(tty)"; fi

commit_with() {
  # commit_with  -- message arrives on stdin
  if [ "$DRY_RUN" = "1" ]; then
    echo "    dry run: not committing"
    return 0
  fi
  setup_hooks
  echo "    committing. You will be asked for your signing passphrase."
  git -c core.hooksPath="$HOOKS" commit -F -
  cleanup_hooks
  echo "    $(git log -1 --format='%h %G? %s')"
}

staged_is_empty() { git diff --cached --quiet; }

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Sanity checks.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
echo "==> Checking the working tree"
if ! git rev-parse --verify --quiet HEAD >/dev/null; then
  echo "ERROR: no HEAD; run tmp/commit-initial.sh first"
  exit 1
fi
if [ "$(git symbolic-ref --short HEAD)" != "master" ]; then
  echo "ERROR: expected branch master, got $(git symbolic-ref --short HEAD)"
  exit 1
fi
# P0-T1 moved HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md with a plain `mv` (its PLAN DECISION:
# the owner forbids staging by the tasks), so NOTHING may be staged; commit 2
# below stages the deletion and the addition together with `git add -A` and
# git's rename detection on the unchanged blob records the move.
staged_unexpected="$(git diff --cached --name-only || true)"
if [ -n "$staged_unexpected" ]; then
  echo "ERROR: unexpected staged paths (unstage with: git reset):"
  echo "$staged_unexpected"
  exit 1
fi
# `.env` is gone from the contract 1.1 ignore list on purpose (P0-T1 deletes
# the file); only tmp/ must be ignored.
git check-ignore -q tmp || { echo "ERROR: tmp is not ignored"; exit 1; }
if [ -n "$(git ls-files --others --exclude-standard | grep -E '(^|/)\.git/' | head -1)" ]; then
  echo "ERROR: an untracked path contains a .git directory"; exit 1
fi

if [ "$GATE" = "1" ]; then
  # =========================================================================
  # COMMIT 5 -- the G0 record (after the first GitHub runs, P0-T4)
  # =========================================================================
  for f in "$PKG/docs/decisions/G0.md" "$PKG/CLAUDE.md"; do
    [ -e "$f" ] || { echo "ERROR: expected $f to exist"; exit 1; }
  done
  echo
  echo "==> 5/5  the G0 gate record"
  git add -- "$PKG/docs/decisions/G0.md" "$PKG/CLAUDE.md"
  if [ -d "$PKG/benchmarks/results" ]; then git add -- "$PKG/benchmarks/results"; fi
  if staged_is_empty; then
    echo "    nothing staged; skipping"
  else
    git diff --cached --stat | tail -1
    commit_with <<'MSGEOF'
docs(webgpu-graph-algorithms): record the G0 gate

docs/decisions/G0.md records what the P0 gate measured (spec 13 row P0):
the default lane green on GitHub (lavapipe acquires an adapter under
GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=any), the deliberate red run (GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=nvidia on
the software lane fails at the gpu-report.js step, no tee), the first
workflow_dispatch of gpu.yml on the gpu-linux-t4 runner with the image facts
(Ubuntu release, glibc, driver, modprobe needed or not, Xvfb needed or not,
libegl1 present), the browser project on SwiftShader (ubuntu-latest) and on
NVIDIA (locally, four flags), the test.env versus define outcome of the
contract's section 9 item 4, the Vitest 3 per-instance launch spelling, the
7.2 formula-table sign-off with the two section-9 corrections, and "no
baseline yet" for benchmarks/results/.

CLAUDE.md "Verified Platform Facts" replaces its "unverified" rows with the
measured answers and the run that measured each.
MSGEOF
  fi
  echo
  git log --oneline -1
  exit 0
fi

for f in "$PKG/src/index.ts" "$PKG/test/setup/gpu.ts" "$PKG/CLAUDE.md" "$PKG/README.md" \
         "$PKG/package.json" "$PKG/vitest.config.ts" "$PKG/scripts/gpu-policy.js" \
         .github/workflows/ci.yml .github/workflows/gpu.yml \
         "$PKG/docs/HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md" "$PKG/docs/research/07-format-api-and-conventions.md" \
         docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-14-webgpu-p0-p3-interfaces.md packages/pnpm-lock.yaml; do
  [ -e "$f" ] || { echo "ERROR: expected $f to exist"; exit 1; }
done
for f in package.json package-lock.json tsconfig.json vitest.config.ts src test examples STRATEGY.md \
         .env .env.example .husky .vscode; do
  [ ! -e "$f" ] || { echo "ERROR: root scaffold path $f still exists (P0-T1 triage incomplete)"; exit 1; }
done
[ ! -e HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md ] || { echo "ERROR: HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md still at the root (P0-T1 move missing)"; exit 1; }
echo "    master, nothing staged, tmp/ ignored, scaffold gone"

# ===========================================================================
# COMMIT 1 -- the triage
# ===========================================================================
echo
echo "==> 1/4  retire the 2025 scaffold"
git add -A -- "${SCAFFOLD_GONE[@]}" "${ROOT_REWRITTEN[@]}"
if staged_is_empty; then
  echo "    nothing staged; skipping"
else
  git diff --cached --stat | tail -1
  commit_with <<'MSGEOF'
chore: retire the 2025 scaffold and rewrite the repository root

Phase P0 of design/webgpu-acceleration-plan.md (section 13; research note 07
section 5) deletes the July 2025 scaffold: the CSRGraph type sketch and the
empty src/ directories (superseded by @graphty/graph-format and the plan's
package layout), the three test setup files and the browser demo tests
(replaced by the package's test/setup and test/browser), the root Vite /
Vitest / ESLint / knip / tsconfig / husky / dotenv tooling (the package is
built and linted by the workspace under packages/, like its siblings), the
example pages, the GitHub workflow that installed Mesa and Xvfb on a runner
without a GPU, and the pre-design STRATEGY / IMPLEMENTATION_CHECKLIST notes
that mention fallbacks the project rule forbids.

The root README.md is rewritten to one screen (what the repository stages,
the package list, pointers to packages/README.md, design/ and
docs/superpowers/plans/) and .gitignore to the plan's list (node_modules,
dist, coverage, tmp/, .tmp/, *.tsbuildinfo, benchmarks/out/,
browser-results.json, gpu-report.json, *.log, .DS_Store). CLAUDE.md ("Never
create fallbacks if WebGPU isn't supported") is kept as is.
MSGEOF
fi

# ===========================================================================
# COMMIT 2 -- the moved docs and the plans
# ===========================================================================
echo
echo "==> 2/4  the moved docs, the research notes and the plans"
git add -A -- HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md "$PKG/docs" docs design
if staged_is_empty; then
  echo "    nothing staged; skipping"
else
  git diff --cached --stat | tail -1
  commit_with <<'MSGEOF'
docs: move the GPU audit under the package; add the P0-P3 contract and plans

HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md moves (git mv, history kept) to
packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/docs/HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md: it is the
verified recipe for the real GPU under headless Chromium on the dev box and
the package CLAUDE.md cites it.

packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/docs/research/ receives the seven research
notes, the three drafts and the review directory (reviews, verdicts, reports
and the probe scripts with their logs) the plan was synthesised from (spec
Q-18), so the package carries its own provenance into the monorepo.

docs/superpowers/plans/ holds the normative interface contract for phases
P0-P3 (every file, signature, WGSL body and test the four phases create,
with the lead's decisions and the open items for the owner) and the
per-phase implementation plans. The spec's Review log gains the owner's
appendices.
MSGEOF
fi

# ===========================================================================
# COMMIT 3 -- manifest, configs, scripts, CI, workspace entries (P0-T2)
# ===========================================================================
echo
echo "==> 3/4  manifest, configs, scripts and the two CI lanes"
git add -- "$PKG/package.json" "$PKG/project.json" "$PKG/tsconfig.json" "$PKG/tsconfig.build.json" \
  "$PKG/tsconfig.strict-consumer.json" "$PKG/eslint.config.js" "$PKG/vitest.config.ts" \
  "$PKG/scripts" "$PKG/benchmarks/run.ts" \
  .github/workflows/ci.yml .github/workflows/gpu.yml \
  packages/pnpm-workspace.yaml packages/knip.config.ts packages/package.json packages/.gitignore \
  packages/pnpm-lock.yaml
if staged_is_empty; then
  echo "    nothing staged; skipping"
else
  git diff --cached --stat | tail -1
  commit_with <<'MSGEOF'
feat(webgpu-graph-algorithms): manifest, configs, scripts and the CI lanes

The package skeleton mirrors graph-io file for file (spec 3.1; contract
section 2): package.json replaces the 0.0.0 placeholder (three exports ".",
"./browser", "./node"; @graphty/graph-format as workspace:^ dependency and

.github/workflows/ci.yml is the default lane (ubuntu-latest: Dawn on

fi

echo

git add -- "$PKG/src" "$PKG/test" "$PKG/README.md" "$PKG/CLAUDE.md"
```

**Depends on / parallel with:** depends on P0-T1, P0-T2, P0-T3 (all three green in the working tree); parallel with nothing -- it is the last task of P0 and it is strictly after T3, so the `CLAUDE.md` edit never races T3's creation of the file (contract 7.1, lead d).

**What G0 is, for a reader who has not seen the spec.** Every phase of this project ends at a gate: a list of tests and MEASURED facts that must be green on both CI lanes before the next phase starts, recorded in `docs/decisions/G<n>.md` (spec 13 rule (a), 10.4). G0 closes P0, the phase that created the package skeleton, the two workflows and the paid GPU lane. Most of G0 is executed by the OWNER (the org admin, apowers313): plan upgrade, runner provisioning, spending limit, repository settings, the first runs, and the sign-off of the ForceAtlas2 formula table (spec 7.2) that every later WGSL body implements. This task prepares every command, template and script, runs the local legs, and fills the record; the owner runs what only the owner can run. Two throwaway branches (`g0-image-probe`, `g0-red-run`) exist for two runs each and are deleted afterwards; nothing on them merges.

**Cost note.** The GPU lane bills $0.052 per minute (spec 12.1). At P0 a `test-gpu` job is short (install, build, a handful of tests, the benchmark stub): budget 8-15 minutes, $0.50-1.00 per run. This task causes at most three paid runs (the push-to-master run after the P0 PR merges, the image-probe dispatch, and the push-to-master run after the record PR merges); the deliberate red run is on `ubuntu-latest` and costs nothing. Every push to `master` triggers `gpu.yml` from now on -- that is the design, not an accident.

**Fill markers.** PLAN DECISION: measured values cannot be known before the runs, so the templates below carry `[[fill: <what>]]` markers; every marker is replaced by the measured literal (never by "n/a" -- a fact that could not be measured is written as "not measured: <why>" with the reason). The task is complete only when `grep -c '\[\[fill:'` prints `0` for `docs/decisions/G0.md` and for `CLAUDE.md` (step 18). The marker spelling is chosen so that a plain grep finds it and nothing else in the repository uses it.

All commands run from `/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms` unless a step says otherwise. Shell snippets assume `bash`.

Run, from the package directory:

```bash
cd /home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms
git -C /home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms status --short | head -40
test -f package.json && grep -c '"name": "@graphty/webgpu-graph-algorithms"' package.json
test -f ../../.github/workflows/gpu.yml && test -f ../../.github/workflows/ci.yml && echo workflows-present
test -f CLAUDE.md && grep -c "unverified" CLAUDE.md
grep -n "^## Verified Platform Facts" CLAUDE.md
test ! -e ../../package-lock.json && test ! -e ../../.github/workflows/test.yml && echo triage-done
test -f docs/HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md && test -d docs/research && echo docs-moved
(cd ../.. && python3 -c "import yaml; yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/gpu.yml')); yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/ci.yml')); print('workflows parse')")
pnpm run build:all && pnpm run lint && (cd .. && pnpm exec knip) && echo GREEN-LOCAL
```

Expected: `git status` lists the T1 deletions (` D package.json`, ` D src/index.ts`, ...), the T2 / T3 additions under `packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/` and `.github/workflows/`, ` M design/webgpu-acceleration-plan.md`, `?? docs/`; `1`; `workflows-present`; a positive count of `unverified` and one `## Verified Platform Facts` heading line; `triage-done`; `docs-moved`; `workflows parse`; `GREEN-LOCAL`. If any line is missing, stop: the dependency task is not finished, and this task does not start.

The `workflows parse` line guards one known hazard that would otherwise make the `GPU` workflow fail at parse time on every event ("Invalid workflow file") after the push of step 11: the contract's `gpu.yml` text (section 2.9) writes `concurrency:` and the `changed` job's `outputs:` as `{ }` flow mappings containing unquoted `${{ ... }}` expressions, and that is not valid YAML -- a parser reads the expression's `{{` as a nested flow mapping (PyYAML: `while parsing a flow mapping ... expected ',' or '}', but got '{'` at the `cancel-in-progress` value and again at `run:`; `ci.yml`'s block-form `concurrency` parses fine). If the command fails on `gpu.yml`, stop and hand P0-T2 the block-form spelling that the red-run file of step 15 uses:

```yaml
concurrency:
    group: gpu-lane-${{ github.event.pull_request.number || github.ref }}
    cancel-in-progress: ${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request' }}
```

and, in the `changed` job,

```yaml
        outputs:
            run: ${{ steps.check.outputs.run }}
```

P0-T2 rewrites those two mappings before the skeleton is committed (step 11); this task does not edit `gpu.yml`. `python3` with PyYAML is on the dev box (`python3 -c "import yaml"` exits 0); if it is not, `pip install --user pyyaml` first -- the check is the cheapest way to see the error GitHub would report only after a push.

Create the helper directory this task uses (gitignored by the root `.gitignore`'s `tmp/` rule, by `packages/.gitignore`, and outside every `tsconfig` include and the ESLint `**/tmp/**` ignore, so nothing lints, type-checks or scans it):

```bash
mkdir -p tmp/g0 && echo ok
```

Expected: `ok`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write and run the local legs script (rows B1, L1-L4, P5, P6)**

Write `/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/g0/local-runs.sh`:

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# G0 local legs (contract 7.1 P0-T4; spec 13 row P0 gate G0, items G0-2 .. G0-4, G0-7, G0-9).
# Runs the four acquisition legs and the two gpu-report policy probes on the dev box, keeps every
# log under tmp/g0/, and prints the lines docs/decisions/G0.md section 3 records.
#
#   bash tmp/g0/local-runs.sh
#
# Never pipes a leg into tee (pipefail is on, but the exit code is read from a subshell anyway).
set -uo pipefail

PKG=/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms
EGL=/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/egl/root/usr/lib/x86_64-linux-gnu
LVP=/usr/share/vulkan/icd.d/lvp_icd.x86_64.json
OUT=$PKG/tmp/g0
mkdir -p "$OUT"
cd "$PKG"
export XDG_RUNTIME_DIR=/tmp
: > "$OUT/exit-codes.txt"

run_leg() {
  # run_leg <name> <command...>  -- stdout to tmp/g0/<name>.out, stderr to tmp/g0/<name>.err
  local name=$1; shift
  echo "==> $name: $*"
  ( "$@" ) > "$OUT/$name.out" 2> "$OUT/$name.err"
  local code=$?
  echo "    exit $code   (tmp/g0/$name.out, tmp/g0/$name.err)"
  echo "$name exit=$code" >> "$OUT/exit-codes.txt"
}

# B1: build, lint (eslint + tsc + the strict-consumer compile), knip at the workspace root.
run_leg build pnpm run build:all
run_leg lint pnpm run lint
run_leg knip bash -c 'cd .. && pnpm exec knip'

# L1: node project on the dev box's NVIDIA adapter (GRAPHTY_GPU_ADAPTER unset -> Dawn picks the discrete GPU).
run_leg node-nvidia env GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=hardware pnpm exec vitest run --project=node
# L2: node project on lavapipe -- the default-lane environment of spec 12.2.
run_leg node-llvmpipe env GRAPHTY_GPU_ADAPTER=llvmpipe GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=any VK_DRIVER_FILES="$LVP" pnpm exec vitest run --project=node
# L3: browser project on NVIDIA with the four flags; the libEGL tree reaches Chromium through GRAPHTY_EGL_LIB_DIR (contract 2.5).
run_leg browser-nvidia env GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU=nvidia GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=nvidia GRAPHTY_EGL_LIB_DIR="$EGL" node scripts/run-browser-project.js
# L4: browser project on SwiftShader (the default lane's flag set).
run_leg browser-swiftshader env GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU=swiftshader GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=any node scripts/run-browser-project.js
# P5: gpu-report under the vendor policy on NVIDIA -> exit 0.
run_leg report-nvidia env GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=nvidia node scripts/gpu-report.js
# P6: gpu-report under the vendor policy on lavapipe -> exit 2 (the local twin of the deliberate red run).
run_leg report-llvmpipe-nvidia env GRAPHTY_GPU_ADAPTER=llvmpipe GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=nvidia VK_DRIVER_FILES="$LVP" node scripts/gpu-report.js

echo
echo "==> exit codes (expected: build 0, lint 0, knip 0, node-nvidia 0, node-llvmpipe 0, browser-nvidia 0, browser-swiftshader 0, report-nvidia 0, report-llvmpipe-nvidia 2)"
cat "$OUT/exit-codes.txt"
echo
echo "==> adapter lines for G0.md rows L1 / L2 (the setup's console.warn goes to stderr)"
grep -h -i -E "vendor|architecture|description|maxBufferSize|maxStorageBufferBindingSize" "$OUT/node-nvidia.err" "$OUT/node-nvidia.out" | head -12
echo "---"
grep -h -i -E "vendor|architecture|description|maxBufferSize|maxStorageBufferBindingSize" "$OUT/node-llvmpipe.err" "$OUT/node-llvmpipe.out" | head -12
echo
echo "==> browser summaries for rows L3 / L4 (run-browser-project.js prints the three counts; 'exit 124' when the close() hang was tolerated)"
grep -h -E "numTotalTests|numPassedTests|numFailedTests|124" "$OUT/browser-nvidia.out" "$OUT/browser-nvidia.err" | head -6
echo "---"
grep -h -E "numTotalTests|numPassedTests|numFailedTests|124" "$OUT/browser-swiftshader.out" "$OUT/browser-swiftshader.err" | head -6
echo
echo "==> gpu-report summaries for rows P5 / P6"
node - "$OUT/report-nvidia.out" "$OUT/report-llvmpipe-nvidia.out" <<'EOF'
const { readFileSync } = require("node:fs");
for (const f of process.argv.slice(2)) {
    let r;
    try { r = JSON.parse(readFileSync(f, "utf8")); } catch (e) { console.log(f, "not JSON:", String(e)); continue; }
    const a = r.adapter || {};
    console.log(f);
    console.log(JSON.stringify({ ok: r.ok, policy: r.policy, vendor: a.vendor, architecture: a.architecture, description: a.description,
        software: a.software, subgroupMinSize: a.subgroupMinSize, subgroupMaxSize: a.subgroupMaxSize, features: a.features,
        deviceLimits: r.deviceLimits, roundTripMs: r.roundTripMs, runnerClass: r.runnerClass,
        nvidiaSmiAvailable: r.nvidiaSmi && r.nvidiaSmi.available, webgpu: r.webgpu, node: r.node, reason: r.reason }, null, 2));
}
EOF
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Measure `test.env` versus `define` in browser mode (rows E1-E3; contract 9 item 4)**

The browser project forwards three variables both through `test.env` and through vite `define` of `import.meta.env.*` (contract 2.5). This step removes the `define` block in a SCRATCH copy of the config and runs a one-assertion probe test; the package's `vitest.config.ts` is not touched.

Write `/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/g0/no-define.mjs`:

```js
// G0 probe (contract 9 item 4): writes tmp/vitest.no-define.config.ts -- vitest.config.ts with the browser
// project's `define` block removed and its include pointed at tmp/env-probe.test.ts -- plus the probe test.
// Nothing under tmp/ is linted, type-checked or committed. Run from the package directory:
//   node tmp/g0/no-define.mjs
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";

const src = readFileSync("vitest.config.ts", "utf8");
const start = src.indexOf("define: {");
if (start < 0) {
    throw new Error("vitest.config.ts: no `define: {` block found");
}
// The block holds three JSON.stringify lines and no nested brace, so the first `},` after it closes it.
const end = src.indexOf("},", start);
if (end < 0) {
    throw new Error("vitest.config.ts: unterminated define block");
}
let out = src.slice(0, start) + src.slice(end + 2);
if (out.includes("\"import.meta.env.GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU\"")) {
    throw new Error("the define block was not removed");
}
const include = "\"test/browser/**/*.test.ts\"";
if (!out.includes(include)) {
    throw new Error("vitest.config.ts: the browser include glob was not found");
}
out = out.replace(include, "\"tmp/env-probe.test.ts\"");
writeFileSync("tmp/vitest.no-define.config.ts", out);

const probe = `/// <reference types="vite/client" />
// G0 env probe: what does the browser tester see in import.meta.env WITHOUT the define block?
describe("G0: import.meta.env forwarding without define", () => {
    it("receives GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU through test.env alone", () => {
        const env = import.meta.env as Record<string, unknown>;
        const seen = { GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU: env.GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU, GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE: env.GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE };
        console.warn(\`G0-ENV-PROBE \${JSON.stringify(seen)}\`);
        expect(env.GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU).toBe("nvidia");
    });
});
`;
writeFileSync("tmp/env-probe.test.ts", probe);
console.log(`wrote tmp/vitest.no-define.config.ts (${src.length - out.length} chars dropped or replaced) and tmp/env-probe.test.ts`);
```

Run the generator, then the probe through the same wrapper the lanes use (the scratch config lives inside the package so `vitest/config` resolves through the package's `node_modules`; the include is the probe file only, so the run makes no WebGPU call and the GPU flag set does not matter -- `nvidia` is passed because the assertion compares against it):

```bash
node tmp/g0/no-define.mjs
GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU=nvidia GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=nvidia GRAPHTY_EGL_LIB_DIR=/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/egl/root/usr/lib/x86_64-linux-gnu \
  timeout -k 10 600 pnpm exec vitest run --project=browser --config tmp/vitest.no-define.config.ts \
  --reporter=default --reporter=json --outputFile=tmp/g0/env-probe-results.json > tmp/g0/env-probe.out 2> tmp/g0/env-probe.err
echo "exit $?"
grep -h "G0-ENV-PROBE" tmp/g0/env-probe.out tmp/g0/env-probe.err | head -2
node -e 'const r = JSON.parse(require("fs").readFileSync("tmp/g0/env-probe-results.json", "utf8")); console.log({ numTotalTests: r.numTotalTests, numPassedTests: r.numPassedTests, numFailedTests: r.numFailedTests });'
```

Expected: `wrote tmp/vitest.no-define.config.ts (...)`; one `G0-ENV-PROBE {"GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU":...,"GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE":...}` line; then EITHER
- outcome A: `numPassedTests: 1, numFailedTests: 0` with `"GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU":"nvidia"` in the probe line -- `test.env` alone reaches `import.meta.env`; the `define` block is redundant; OR
- outcome B: `numFailedTests: 1` with `"GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU":` absent / `""` in the probe line -- `test.env` does NOT reach the browser tester; `define` is necessary.

An `exit 124` with the counts above is the `browser.close()` hang and does not change the verdict. If `test/setup/browser.ts` itself throws before the probe runs (it reads the two `import.meta.env` keys at module level and may reject an undefined `GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU`), that is outcome B as well -- the value did not arrive -- and the thrown line is the E2 evidence. Fill E1 (A or B), E2 (the probe line verbatim and pass / fail) and E3 (the consequence text of the template's row for that outcome) in G0.md section 7, and U2 in section 10 (`not needed` for B; for A the follow-up commit hash once the owner makes it after G0). PLAN DECISION: in outcome B the browser project's `env:` line stays as written (it is inert for browser code but harmless, and removing it would edit P0-T2's file for nothing); in outcome A the `define` block is removed in a follow-up commit after G0, not in P0, because `vitest.config.ts` is P0-T2's file and the outcome is only known now.

Remove the two scratch files so the package's `tmp/` holds only the G0 logs:

```bash
rm -f tmp/vitest.no-define.config.ts tmp/env-probe.test.ts && ls tmp
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `tmp/commit-p0.sh` (root `tmp/`; the owner runs it, never this task)**

The script has two stages: `skeleton` (four commits: the contract + plan documents, the triage, the package infrastructure, the source skeleton -- T1 / T2 / T3's work) and `record` (one commit: this task's G0 record, the CLAUDE.md facts, the spec's Review-log appendix). Each stage accepts `--dry-run`. No hooks exist in this repository (the scaffold's `.husky/` is untracked and deleted by T1), so commits run plain `git commit -F -`. GPG signing prompts once per commit. The script never pushes.

Write `/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/commit-p0.sh`:

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Commit phase P0 of the WebGPU acceleration plan (contract 7.1; spec 13 row P0).
#
#   bash tmp/commit-p0.sh skeleton            # four commits: docs, triage, package infrastructure, src skeleton
#   bash tmp/commit-p0.sh skeleton --dry-run  # stage each commit in turn and show what would be committed
#   bash tmp/commit-p0.sh record              # one commit: the G0 record, CLAUDE.md facts, the spec's Review-log appendix
#   bash tmp/commit-p0.sh record --dry-run
#
# The only prompt is the commit signing passphrase, once per commit. No hooks run: this repository
# has none. The script stages explicit pathspecs only, so an unrelated file in the tree is never
# swept in; it prints what is left untracked at the end. It never pushes.

set -euo pipefail

REPO="/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms"
PKG="packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms"
STAGE="${1:-}"
DRY_RUN=0
[ "${2:-}" = "--dry-run" ] && DRY_RUN=1

if [ "$STAGE" != "skeleton" ] && [ "$STAGE" != "record" ]; then
  echo "usage: bash tmp/commit-p0.sh skeleton|record [--dry-run]"
  exit 2
fi

cd "$REPO"

# Let gpg-agent find the terminal so pinentry can prompt here.
if tty -s; then export GPG_TTY="$(tty)"; fi

commit_with() {
  # commit_with  -- the message arrives on stdin
  if [ "$DRY_RUN" = "1" ]; then
    echo "    dry run: not committing (the staged files stay staged; run 'git reset -q' to undo)"
    return 0
  fi
  echo "    committing. You will be asked for your signing passphrase."
  git commit -q -F -
  echo "    $(git log -1 --format='%h %G? %s')"
}

staged_is_empty() { git diff --cached --quiet; }

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Sanity checks.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
echo "==> Checking the working tree"
if [ -z "$(git status --short | head -1)" ]; then
  echo "ERROR: nothing to commit. Already run?  git log --oneline -6"
  exit 1
fi
if ! git diff --cached --quiet; then
  echo "    something is already staged (a 'git mv' from the triage, most likely):"
  git diff --cached --name-status | head -10
  echo "    unstaging it; every commit below re-stages its own pathspecs"
  git reset -q
fi
branch="$(git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD)"
if [ "$STAGE" = "skeleton" ] && [ "$branch" = "master" ]; then
  echo "ERROR: on master. The P0 skeleton goes to a branch:  git checkout -b p0-skeleton"
  exit 1
fi
if [ "$STAGE" = "record" ] && [ "$branch" = "master" ]; then
  echo "ERROR: on master. The record goes to a branch:  git checkout -b p0-g0-record master"
  exit 1
fi
echo "    branch $branch, clean index, changes present"

# ===========================================================================
# STAGE skeleton -- four commits
# ===========================================================================
if [ "$STAGE" = "skeleton" ]; then
  for f in "$PKG/package.json" "$PKG/vitest.config.ts" "$PKG/src/index.ts" "$PKG/CLAUDE.md" \
           ".github/workflows/ci.yml" ".github/workflows/gpu.yml" "$PKG/docs/HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md" \
           "docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-14-webgpu-p0-p3-interfaces.md"; do
    [ -e "$f" ] || { echo "ERROR: expected $f to exist"; exit 1; }
  done
  for f in "package.json" "package-lock.json" ".github/workflows/test.yml" "HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md" "STRATEGY.md"; do
    [ ! -e "$f" ] || { echo "ERROR: the triage left $f at the repository root"; exit 1; }
  done

  # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  echo
  echo "==> 1/4  the contract, the P0 plan and the spec's Review-log entry"
  git add -A -- docs/superpowers/plans design/webgpu-acceleration-plan.md
  if staged_is_empty; then
    echo "    nothing staged; skipping"
  else
    git diff --cached --stat | tail -3
    commit_with <<'MSGEOF'
docs: add the P0-P3 interface contract and the P0 plan

docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-14-webgpu-p0-p3-interfaces.md is the normative
interface contract for phases P0-P3 of design/webgpu-acceleration-plan.md:
the file tree with owners, every config file, every TypeScript declaration,
the normative WGSL bodies, the test and benchmark contracts, and the task map.
The per-phase P0 plan sits beside it. The spec gains the Review-log entry
that lists the four factual corrections the contract makes to 7.2, 3.5 and
4.1, pending the owner's confirmation at the G0 sign-off.
MSGEOF
  fi

  # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  echo
  echo "==> 2/4  scaffold triage (deletions, root README / .gitignore, the report moved, research notes copied)"
  git add -A -- .env.example .github/workflows/test.yml .vscode examples eslint.config.js knip.json \
    package-lock.json package.json src test tsconfig.json vite.config.ts vitest.config.ts \
    STRATEGY.md IMPLEMENTATION_CHECKLIST.md README.md .gitignore HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md \
    "$PKG/docs/HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md" "$PKG/docs/research"
  if staged_is_empty; then
    echo "    nothing staged; skipping"
  else
    git diff --cached --stat | tail -3
    echo "    renames: $(git diff --cached --name-status -M | grep -c '^R' || true)"
    commit_with <<'MSGEOF'
chore: delete the 2025 scaffold and move the headless GPU report under the package

The repository root held an npm scaffold from 2025 (root package.json and
lockfile, src/ with a CSRGraph type nothing used, a Vitest 2 browser config
that forced SwiftShader, an Xvfb workflow on Node 18/20, examples, husky,
strategy and checklist documents). None of it is the package: the package
lives under packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms in the pnpm workspace beside
graph-format and graph-io. Every scaffold file is deleted; the root README
now says what the repository stages and points at packages/, design/ and
docs/superpowers/plans/; .gitignore lists the build, coverage, tmp and
run-output paths the workspace produces.

HEADLESS_GPU_REPORT.md moves to packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/docs/ (a
rename, so its history follows). The seven research notes, the three drafts
and the review directory from tmp/webgpu-plan are copied to
packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/docs/research/ so the plan's citations
resolve inside the repository.
MSGEOF
  fi

  # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  echo
  echo "==> 3/4  package manifest, configs, scripts, workflows, workspace entries, lockfile"
  git add -A -- .github/workflows/ci.yml .github/workflows/gpu.yml \
    packages/pnpm-workspace.yaml packages/knip.config.ts packages/package.json packages/.gitignore packages/pnpm-lock.yaml \
    "$PKG/package.json" "$PKG/project.json" "$PKG/tsconfig.json" "$PKG/tsconfig.build.json" \
    "$PKG/tsconfig.strict-consumer.json" "$PKG/eslint.config.js" "$PKG/vitest.config.ts" \
    "$PKG/scripts" "$PKG/benchmarks/run.ts"
  if staged_is_empty; then
    echo "    nothing staged; skipping"
  else
    git diff --cached --stat | tail -3
    commit_with <<'MSGEOF'
build(webgpu-graph-algorithms): manifest, configs, scripts and the two CI lanes

The 0.0.0 trusted-publishing placeholder becomes the package: the manifest
with the root, ./browser and ./node entries, the Nx project, the tsconfig
trio (lint, emit with stripInternal, the strict-consumer compile of the
type tests against dist), the package-local ESLint config with the layer
zones, and the Vitest config with the node, node-limits and browser
projects (browser flags selected by GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU, the three

scripts/ carries the bundle build shared with graph-io, gpu-policy.js (the

exit on a policy violation), run-browser-project.js (the timeout wrapper
with the exit-124 rule) and the bench-compare stub. benchmarks/run.ts is
the P0 stub.

.github/workflows/ci.yml is the required default lane on ubuntu-latest
(Dawn on Mesa lavapipe, Chromium on SwiftShader). gpu.yml is the paid GPU
lane on the hosted gpu-linux-t4 runner in its own workflow, never a job of

  fi

  # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

  git add -A -- "$PKG/src" "$PKG/test" "$PKG/README.md" "$PKG/CLAUDE.md"
  if staged_is_empty; then
    echo "    nothing staged; skipping"
  else
    git diff --cached --stat | tail -3
    commit_with <<'MSGEOF'

  fi
fi

# ===========================================================================
# STAGE record -- one commit
# ===========================================================================
if [ "$STAGE" = "record" ]; then
  for f in "$PKG/docs/decisions/G0.md" "$PKG/CLAUDE.md" "design/webgpu-acceleration-plan.md"; do
```

There is no API for spending limits; the setting is a UI step. Two spellings exist depending on which billing platform the organization is on:

- New billing platform (Settings > "Billing and licensing"): Budgets and alerts > "New budget": Budget type "Product", Product "Actions", Scope: this organization (or narrowed to the repository `webgpu-graph-algorithms`), Budget amount `50`, "Stop usage when budget limit is reached" ON, Alerts ON with the 75% threshold selected (90% and 100% may stay selected too), Alert recipients: the owner's e-mail; Create budget.
- Legacy billing (Settings > "Billing and plans"): Spending limits > "Actions & Packages" > "Limit spending" `50` USD > Save; "Email alerts" ON, threshold 75%.

Verify by re-opening the page and reading the saved values back. Record O6 in G0.md with: which platform, the budget name / type, the amount, stop-at-limit on / off, the alert thresholds, the recipients, the date. A raised limit later is "raised deliberately, never silently" (spec 12.2): it goes into a later gate record.

The repository's default workflow permissions are `write` today (`gh api .../actions/permissions/workflow` printed `{"default_workflow_permissions":"write","can_approve_pull_request_reviews":false}` on 2026-09-15). Both workflows also declare `permissions: { contents: read }` at the top, but the repository default must be read-only so that a future workflow without a `permissions:` key does not inherit `write` (spec 12.2).

```bash
gh api -X PUT /repos/graphty-org/webgpu-graph-algorithms/actions/permissions/workflow --input - <<'EOF'
{ "default_workflow_permissions": "read", "can_approve_pull_request_reviews": false }
EOF

gh api -X PUT /repos/graphty-org/webgpu-graph-algorithms/actions/permissions/fork-pr-contributor-approval --input - <<'EOF'
{ "approval_policy": "all_external_contributors" }
EOF
gh api /repos/graphty-org/webgpu-graph-algorithms/actions/permissions/fork-pr-contributor-approval
gh api -X POST /repos/graphty-org/webgpu-graph-algorithms/labels --input - <<'EOF'
{ "name": "gpu", "color": "76B900", "description": "Maintainer-applied: run the paid GPU lane (gpu.yml) on this same-repo PR" }
EOF
gh api /repos/graphty-org/webgpu-graph-algorithms/labels --jq '.[] | select(.name == "gpu") | {name, color, description}'
```

Expected: `{"default_workflow_permissions":"read","can_approve_pull_request_reviews":false}`; `{"approval_policy":"all_external_contributors"}` (the PUT is accepted only once step 7 made the repository public; the UI equivalent is Settings > Actions > General > "Approval for running fork pull request workflows from contributors" > "Require approval for all external contributors"); `{"name":"gpu","color":"76B900","description":"Maintainer-applied: ..."}`. The installed `gh` 2.4.0 has no `gh label` command, hence the REST call. Record O7, O8 (the GET responses verbatim) and O9.

Do NOT apply the `gpu` label to the P0 PR of step 11: a labelled same-repo PR is a paid run, and the P0 PR's GPU-lane evidence comes from the `master` push run and the dispatches of steps 13-15 instead.

```markdown
Phase P0 of `design/webgpu-acceleration-plan.md` (section 13 row P0), implemented per
```

Wait for the CI run and collect it. Write `/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/g0/collect-run.sh`:

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Saves one GitHub Actions run's metadata, jobs / steps, full log and artifacts under
# tmp/g0/runs/<label>/ and prints the lines docs/decisions/G0.md records (sections 4, 5, 6).
#
#   bash tmp/g0/collect-run.sh <run-id> <label>      e.g.  bash tmp/g0/collect-run.sh 1234567890 ci-pr
#
# Uses the REST API for jobs and steps because the installed gh (2.4.0) has no `jobs` JSON field.
set -euo pipefail
REPO=graphty-org/webgpu-graph-algorithms
RUN=${1:?run id}
LABEL=${2:?label}
OUT=/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/g0/runs/$LABEL
mkdir -p "$OUT"

gh run view "$RUN" --repo "$REPO" --json databaseId,url,headBranch,headSha,event,status,conclusion,createdAt,updatedAt > "$OUT/run.json"
gh api "/repos/$REPO/actions/runs/$RUN/jobs" > "$OUT/jobs.json"
gh run view "$RUN" --repo "$REPO" --log > "$OUT/run.log" || echo "(log not available yet)"
gh run download "$RUN" --repo "$REPO" -D "$OUT/artifacts" 2>/dev/null || echo "(no artifacts)"

echo "== run";   jq -r '"\(.url)  event=\(.event)  branch=\(.headBranch)  sha=\(.headSha[0:12])  conclusion=\(.conclusion)  created=\(.createdAt)  updated=\(.updatedAt)"' "$OUT/run.json"
echo "== jobs";  jq -r '.jobs[] | "\(.name): \(.conclusion)  \(.started_at) -> \(.completed_at)  runner=\(.runner_name // "-")  group=\(.runner_group_name // "-")"' "$OUT/jobs.json"
echo "== steps"; jq -r '.jobs[] | .name as $j | .steps[] | "\($j) / \(.name): \(.conclusion)  \(.started_at) -> \(.completed_at)"' "$OUT/jobs.json"
echo "== runner image (Set up job)"; grep -E "Runner Image|Image:|Version:|Runner name|Runner group name|Machine name" "$OUT/run.log" | sed 's/^.*\t//' | sort -u | head -12 || true
echo "== image facts (probe step, when present)"; sed -n '/== os/,/== sudo/p' "$OUT/run.log" | sed 's/^.*\t//' | head -60 || true
echo "== modules after Driver up (probe, when present)"; grep -E "Modules after Driver up" -A2 "$OUT/run.log" | sed 's/^.*\t//' | head -6 || true
echo "== nvidia-smi (Driver up)"; grep -E "Driver Version|Tesla|NVIDIA-SMI|CUDA Version" "$OUT/run.log" | sed 's/^.*\t//' | head -6 || true
echo "== adapter lines (node setup, canary)"; grep -E "vendor=|vendor|architecture|description" "$OUT/run.log" | grep -v -i "node_modules" | sed 's/^.*\t//' | head -12 || true
echo "== browser summary"; grep -E "numTotalTests|numPassedTests|numFailedTests|exit 124|Process completed with exit code" "$OUT/run.log" | sed 's/^.*\t//' | head -12 || true
echo "== gpu-report.json"
f=$(find "$OUT/artifacts" -name gpu-report.json 2>/dev/null | head -1)
if [ -n "$f" ]; then
  jq '{ok, policy, adapter: {vendor: .adapter.vendor, architecture: .adapter.architecture, device: .adapter.device, description: .adapter.description, software: .adapter.software, subgroupMinSize: .adapter.subgroupMinSize, subgroupMaxSize: .adapter.subgroupMaxSize, features: .adapter.features}, deviceLimits, roundTripMs, runnerClass, nvidiaSmi: {available: .nvidiaSmi.available, maxUtilization: .nvidiaSmi.maxUtilization, maxMemoryUsedMiB: .nvidiaSmi.maxMemoryUsedMiB}, webgpu, node, reason}' "$f"
else
  echo "(no gpu-report.json in the artifacts)"
fi
```

```markdown
| # | Contract correction (spec statement it corrects) | Decision | Consequence of each choice |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `compat: "networkx"` pulls toward the ORIGIN: NetworkX 3.4.2 `forceatlas2_layout` computes `-gravity * mass * pos / |pos|` (`layout.py` 1466-1471), so the contract compiles `GRAVITY_CENTER = 1` in that mode (spec 7.2 gravity-centre row, third column, and Q-1's "centroid (port, NetworkX)" wording) | ACCEPTED / RE-DECIDED: | accepted: the row and Q-1 are amended in the Review log. re-decided (centroid in networkx mode): the NetworkX trajectory fixtures are no oracle for gravity in that mode; P3-T4's fixture plan changes before P3 starts |
| 2 | The per-node factor in `SWING_MODE = 1` uses `swing_i = m_i |F_i|` (`layout.py` 1497: `swinging = mass * |update|`); only the accumulated GLOBAL sums mix positions (line 1483) (spec 7.2 "Local speed / apply" row's NetworkX column and 7.11's local factor; the "Swing / traction per node" row and 7.10 -- the inputs of the global sums, K3's epilogue -- are already right) | ACCEPTED / RE-DECIDED: | accepted: the "Local speed / apply" row and 7.11 are amended; the "Swing / traction per node" row and 7.10 stay. re-decided (`m_i |p_i - F_i|`): the same fixtures cannot pin the controller; P3-T2's K5 body and P3-T4 change |
```

Two failure modes and what to do:
- The job sits `queued` for more than ~10 minutes: the runner is not `Ready`, the group does not include the repository, or `runs-on: gpu-linux-t4` does not match the runner's name -- re-check step 8's verification commands; a run that never starts is cancelled by GitHub after 24 hours (spec 12.1), so cancel it yourself (`gh run cancel <id>`) once the cause is fixed and let the next push or a dispatch replace it.
- `Browser smoke on NVIDIA` is red while everything before it is green: headless Chromium did not pick the T4 (the `webgpu-check` test's vendor assertion fails, or `requestAdapter` returns a fallback). That is the Xvfb question of spec 12.2; step 14's variant B answers it. Rows F8 / U1 record the answer; the run itself still counts for M1's other rows, and G0-16 ("GPU lane green on master") is satisfied by the `master` run after U1's follow-up commit.

- [ ] **Step 14 (owner): the image-probe dispatch on the throwaway branch `g0-image-probe` (rows M2, F1-F3, F5-F9)**

PLAN DECISION: `gpu.yml` is P0-T2's file and the "was `modprobe` needed" question needs a look at the module list BEFORE the `Driver up` step runs, so the probe lives on a branch that is never merged: `master`'s `gpu.yml` plus one step before `Driver up` and one after it. `workflow_dispatch` on a branch works because the workflow exists on `master` since step 12 (GitHub lists dispatchable workflows from the default branch and runs the branch's version of the file).

Write `/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/g0/make-probe-branch.mjs`:

```js
// G0 probe (spec 12.2, 12.4 item 5): edits .github/workflows/gpu.yml IN THE CURRENT BRANCH by inserting the
// "Image facts" step before "Driver up" and the "Modules after Driver up" step after it. With --xvfb it also
// wraps the browser step in xvfb-run (variant B). Run from the repository root on the g0-image-probe branch:
//   node packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/g0/make-probe-branch.mjs [--xvfb]
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";

const file = ".github/workflows/gpu.yml";
const src = readFileSync(file, "utf8");
const xvfb = process.argv.includes("--xvfb");

const checkoutLine = "            - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n            - name: Driver up";
if (!src.includes(checkoutLine)) {
    throw new Error(`${file}: the checkout / Driver up anchor was not found; insert the steps by hand at those lines`);
}
const probeStep = `            - uses: actions/checkout@v4
            - name: Image facts (G0 probe -- this step exists only on the g0-image-probe branch)
              run: |
                  set +e
                  echo "== os";      grep -E '^(PRETTY_NAME|VERSION_ID)=' /etc/os-release
                  echo "== glibc";   ldd --version | head -1
                  echo "== kernel";  uname -r
                  echo "== nvidia modules before Driver up"; lsmod | grep -E '^nvidia' || echo "no nvidia module loaded"
                  echo "== nvidia-smi before Driver up"; nvidia-smi --query-gpu=name,driver_version --format=csv,noheader || echo "nvidia-smi failed before Driver up (exit $?)"
                  echo "== libegl1"; dpkg-query -W -f='\${Package} \${Version} \${Status}\\n' libegl1 || echo "libegl1 absent"
                  echo "== libEGL.so.1"; ldconfig -p | grep -F libEGL.so.1 || echo "libEGL.so.1 not in the ldconfig cache"
                  echo "== vulkan icds"; ls -1 /usr/share/vulkan/icd.d /etc/vulkan/icd.d 2>/dev/null || echo "no icd directory"
                  echo "== mesa";    dpkg-query -W -f='\${Package} \${Version}\\n' mesa-vulkan-drivers libvulkan1 || echo "mesa-vulkan-drivers or libvulkan1 absent"
                  echo "== xvfb";    command -v xvfb-run || echo "xvfb-run absent"
                  echo "== display"; echo "DISPLAY=\${DISPLAY:-unset}"
                  echo "== sudo";    sudo -n true && echo "passwordless sudo ok" || echo "no passwordless sudo"
                  exit 0
            - name: Driver up`;
let out = src.replace(checkoutLine, probeStep);

const driverLine = "              run: sudo modprobe nvidia nvidia_uvm || true; nvidia-smi\n";
if (!out.includes(driverLine)) {
    throw new Error(`${file}: the Driver up run line was not found`);
}
out = out.replace(driverLine, `${driverLine}            - name: Modules after Driver up (G0 probe)
              run: lsmod | grep -E '^nvidia' || echo "no nvidia module loaded after Driver up"
`);

if (xvfb) {
    const browserLine = "              run: node scripts/run-browser-project.js ";
    if (!out.includes(browserLine)) {
        throw new Error(`${file}: the browser smoke run line was not found`);
    }
    out = out.replace(
        browserLine,
        "              run: sudo apt-get update && sudo apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends xvfb && xvfb-run -a node scripts/run-browser-project.js ",
    );
}
writeFileSync(file, out);
console.log(`${file}: probe steps inserted${xvfb ? " (variant B: xvfb-run around the browser smoke)" : ""}`);
```

- [ ] **Step 15 (owner): the deliberate red run on the throwaway branch `g0-red-run` (rows R1-R6, S7)**

PLAN DECISION: the red run replaces `gpu.yml` wholesale on a branch that is never merged: the `test-gpu` job moves to `ubuntu-latest` with the lavapipe install of `ci.yml` in place of `Driver up`, `GRAPHTY_GPU_ADAPTER=llvmpipe` and `VK_DRIVER_FILES` in the job environment, and `GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE=nvidia` UNCHANGED. On that machine Dawn finds lavapipe (a software adapter), so `scripts/gpu-report.js` must exit `2` and the step must be red -- which it can be only because the step reads the exit code (`> gpu-report.json && cat ...`, no `| tee`). A missing ICD would make the script exit `3` instead; that is why lavapipe is installed and selected explicitly: the run must prove the POLICY failure mode, not a missing-driver one. The run costs nothing (standard runner).

Write `/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/g0/gpu-red-run.yml` (the contract's `gpu.yml` with deltas 3, 4 and 7 applied and the four red-run edits marked `RED RUN`):

```yaml
# .github/workflows/gpu.yml -- G0 DELIBERATE RED RUN (throwaway branch g0-red-run, never merged; spec 12.4 item 5)
# The GPU lane's own file with four edits: runs-on ubuntu-latest, lavapipe installed and selected, no Driver up step.
# GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE stays `nvidia`, so the Adapter report step MUST fail with exit 2 and every later step is skipped.
name: GPU
on:
    push: { branches: [master] }
    pull_request: { types: [labeled, synchronize] }
    schedule: [{ cron: "17 6 * * *" }]          # nightly
    workflow_dispatch:
permissions: { contents: read }
concurrency:                                     # block form: an unquoted ${{ }} inside a { } flow mapping is not valid YAML (see the step-1 precondition note)
    group: gpu-lane-${{ github.event.pull_request.number || github.ref }}
    cancel-in-progress: ${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request' }}

jobs:
    changed:                                     # nightly cost guard: skip when master has not moved since the last green GPU run (12.6)
        runs-on: ubuntu-latest
        outputs:
            run: ${{ steps.check.outputs.run }}
        steps:
            - id: check
              env: { GH_TOKEN: "${{ github.token }}" }
              run: |
                  if [ "${{ github.event_name }}" != "schedule" ]; then echo run=true >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"; exit 0; fi
                  last=$(gh run list --workflow GPU --branch master --status success --limit 1 --json headSha --jq '.[0].headSha')
                  [ "$last" = "${{ github.sha }}" ] && echo run=false >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT" || echo run=true >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    test-gpu:
        name: Test (NVIDIA T4, GitHub-hosted)
        needs: changed
        if: >-
            needs.changed.outputs.run == 'true' &&
            (github.event_name != 'pull_request' ||
             (github.event.pull_request.head.repo.full_name == github.repository &&
              contains(github.event.pull_request.labels.*.name, 'gpu')))
        runs-on: ubuntu-latest                   # RED RUN: the software lane instead of gpu-linux-t4
        timeout-minutes: 45
        env:
            GRAPHTY_GPU_REQUIRE: nvidia                 # a software adapter fails the job -- THE claim this run proves
            GRAPHTY_BROWSER_GPU: nvidia
            GRAPHTY_RUNNER_CLASS: g0-red-run            # RED RUN: never gpu-linux-t4, so no baseline is ever compared against this run
            GRAPHTY_GPU_ADAPTER: llvmpipe               # RED RUN: Dawn picks the software adapter deterministically
            VK_DRIVER_FILES: /usr/share/vulkan/icd.d/lvp_icd.x86_64.json   # RED RUN
            XDG_RUNTIME_DIR: /tmp
        defaults: { run: { working-directory: packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms, shell: bash } }
        steps:
            - uses: actions/checkout@v4
            - name: Install Mesa lavapipe (RED RUN -- replaces the Driver up step; a missing ICD would exit 3, not the policy's 2)
              run: sudo apt-get update && sudo apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends mesa-vulkan-drivers libvulkan1
            - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
              with: { package_json_file: packages/package.json }
            - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
              with: { node-version: 22.x, cache: pnpm, cache-dependency-path: packages/pnpm-lock.yaml }
            - run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
              working-directory: packages
            - run: pnpm run build
              working-directory: packages
            - name: Adapter report (fails loudly on a software adapter; samples GPU utilisation for 10 s)
              run: node scripts/gpu-report.js > gpu-report.json && cat gpu-report.json   # no pipe into tee: the report's non-zero exit must reach the step
            - name: graph-format GPU audit on NVIDIA (canary)
              working-directory: packages/graph-format
              run: pnpm exec vitest run test/audit/gpu-upload.test.ts 2>&1 | tee canary.log && grep -q "adapter vendor=nvidia" canary.log
            - run: pnpm exec vitest run --project=node --project=node-limits
            - run: GRAPHTY_GPU_NO_SUBGROUPS=1 pnpm exec vitest run --project=node
            - name: Cache Playwright browsers
              id: pw
              uses: actions/cache@v4
              with: { path: ~/.cache/ms-playwright, key: "playwright-${{ runner.os }}-${{ hashFiles('packages/pnpm-lock.yaml') }}" }
            - if: steps.pw.outputs.cache-hit != 'true'
              run: pnpm exec playwright install chromium --with-deps
            - name: Browser smoke on NVIDIA
              run: node scripts/run-browser-project.js
            - run: pnpm run bench
            - run: node scripts/bench-compare.js
            - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
              if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
              with: { name: "gpu-results-${{ github.run_id }}", path: "packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/gpu-report.json\npackages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/benchmarks/out/", retention-days: 90, overwrite: true }

    gpu-nightly-report:
        needs: test-gpu
        if: always() && github.event_name == 'schedule' && needs.test-gpu.result != 'success'
        runs-on: ubuntu-latest
        permissions: { issues: write }
        steps:
            - uses: actions/github-script@v7
              with: { script: "/* open or refresh the 'GPU lane nightly' tracking issue; only after two consecutive nightly failures (the previous night's result is read from the issue body), 12.6 */" }
```

Write the splice script `/home/apowers/Projects/webgpu-graph-algorithms/packages/webgpu-graph-algorithms/tmp/g0/splice-facts.mjs`:

```js
// Replaces the "## Verified Platform Facts" section of CLAUDE.md (from its heading up to the next "## " heading
// or the end of the file) with the contents of tmp/g0/verified-platform-facts.md. Run from the package directory:
//   node tmp/g0/splice-facts.mjs
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";

const file = "CLAUDE.md";
const heading = "## Verified Platform Facts";
const src = readFileSync(file, "utf8");
const replacement = `${readFileSync("tmp/g0/verified-platform-facts.md", "utf8").trimEnd()}\n\n`;
if (!replacement.startsWith(`${heading}\n`)) {
    throw new Error("tmp/g0/verified-platform-facts.md must start with the heading line");
}
if (/\[\[fill:/.test(replacement)) {
    throw new Error("tmp/g0/verified-platform-facts.md still has [[fill: markers; fill them from docs/decisions/G0.md first");
}
const start = src.indexOf(`\n${heading}\n`);
if (start < 0) {
    throw new Error(`${file}: heading "${heading}" not found (P0-T3 must have named the section exactly so)`);
}
const sectionStart = start + 1;
const next = src.indexOf("\n## ", sectionStart + heading.length);
const sectionEnd = next < 0 ? src.length : next + 1;
const out = `${src.slice(0, sectionStart)}${replacement}${src.slice(sectionEnd)}`;
writeFileSync(file, out);
console.log(`${file}: replaced ${sectionEnd - sectionStart} chars of the section with ${replacement.length}`);
```
